## Document 208: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 변하지 않은 것SK하이닉스(000660) 변하지 않은 것변하지 않은 것 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 변하지 않은 것SK하이닉스(000660) 변하지 않은 것변하지 않은 것 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-07-30

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

00

SK 하이닉스 (000660/KS) 
변하지 않은 것 
2Q26 영업이익 61 조원 (+19% QoQ) 기록 
SK하이닉스의 2Q26 영업이익은 61조원 (+19% QoQ)으로 SK증권 예상치 (60.7
조원)에 부합했으나, 시장컨센서스 대비 소폭 하회했다. 주요 원인은 고부가 메모리의 
본격 출하가 3Q26으로 이연된 영향이다. 출하는 DRAM +9%, NAND +14%, 판가
는 DRAM +29%, NAND +54%, 영업이익은 DRAM 46조원, NAND 15조원으로 
추정된다. 영업이익률은 DRAM 79%, NAND 69%로 HBM4 본격화 이전, 경쟁사대
비 높은 HBM 비중에 따른 불리한 믹스에도 불구하고, 여전히 업계 최고 수익성이다.

업황 강세 지속 
SK 증권은 시장 우려에도 불구하고, 증익 기조는 전혀 변하지 않았다고 판단한다. 현재 
업계의 재고 수준은 공급자 3 주, 하이퍼스케일러 3주, 채널 5-7주 수준으로 정상 범
주 대비 절반 수준에 불과하며, 수요 충족률은 70% 미만으로 파악된다. LTA효과는 
성장 종료가 아닌, 수요 예측력 제고와 실적 지속성 제고, 이중시장 효과에 따른 비 
LTA 및 현물 시장의 가격 탄력성 제고이다. 제한적 공간 여력, HBM 및 TLC 강세에 
따른 웨이퍼 비트 효율 감소 등을 AI 사이클에서 구조적 현상이며, 공급 부족은 2027
년에 더욱 심화될 것이다. 메모리 가격 상승과 전통 세트 위축에 따른 가격 저항이 목격
되고 있으나, 공급자들은 AI 서버 향 생산 능력 배분 확대로 대응하고 있다. 공급자들
의 세트향 비중이 30% 이하로 하락하는 과정에 있다는 점, AI 투자 기조 지속을 감안
하면, 세트에서 나타나는 신호를 전체화 시키는 것은 성급한 오류이다. SK 하이닉스의 
3Q26 DRAM 가격 역시 시장 우려와 달리 +19% QoQ로 예상을 상회할 것이다.

목표주가, 실적 전망 모두 유지 
SK증권은 SK하이닉스에 대한 목표주가 400만원을 유지한다. 시장 금리 상승에 대
한 우려와 CDS 프리미엄 상승 등 AI 밸류에 불리한 환경에도 불구하고 목표주가를 유
지하는 이유는 다음과 같다. ① 26, 27년 실적 전망 유지 (각각 274조원, 425조원), 
② AI 설비투자에 대한 지속성 (낮은 급락 가능성; 과거 수요와 다른 성격, 명분), ③ 
LTA를 통한 이익 가시성, 지속성, 창출력 제고, ④ 하반기 주주환원 시작이다. 과거와 
다른 업황을 인정받기 위해서는 주주환원이 가장 중요하며, ‘실적 성장률’이 아닌 ‘이익 
창출력, 지속력’을 기반으로 평가받는 중요한 변곡점이 될 것이다. 26, 27, 28년 SK하
이닉스의 FCF는 150조원, 261조원, 346조원 수준으로 전망되며, 주주환원의 가시화
는 순현금 100조원 달성이 예상되는 3Q26 실적 발표 또는 그 이전을 기대한다.

2026-07-30

반도체. 한동희 / donghee.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-8826

## Document 219: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 2Q26 Review_ 무언가 시작되는 느낌두산에너빌리티(034020) 2Q26 Review_ 무언가 시작되는 느낌2Q26 Review_ 무언가 시작되는 느낌 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 2Q26 Review_ 무언가 시작되는 느낌두산에너빌리티(034020) 2Q26 Review_ 무언가 시작되는 느낌2Q26 Review_ 무언가 시작되는 느낌 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-07-28
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 2Q26 Review: 무언가 시작되는 느낌  
밥캣의 관세 환급 효과 덕분에 서프라이즈 
2Q26 연결 영업이익은 3,143억원(+15.9% YoY) 을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,629억원)
를 상회했다. 두산밥캣이 대미 상호관세 환급 효과로 영업이익이 개선(+40.5% YoY)
된 것이 주요 원인이었다. 에너빌리티 부문 영업이익 974억원(+5.4% YoY) 은 예상 
수준이었다. 수주는 4.3조원, 상반기 누적 7.1조원(+87.4% YoY) 을 달성했다. 국내
외 가스터빈 수주와 중동 가스복합 EPC 수주가 주요 동력이었다.

2026년, AP1000 발주가 시작될 가능성에 주목 
당사의 2026년 수주 전망치를 17.9조원으로 상향한다(vs 직전 보고서 15.7조원). 
이는 상반기 가스터빈 수주 호조와 AP1000 기자재 수주 가능성을 반영한 것이다. 
미국 DOE는 7월 'American Nuclear Supply Chain Loans ’을 통해 
Westinghouse에게 장납기 기자재 발주를 위한 자금을 마련해줬다. 총 10기 대형원
전을 위한 이번 자금은 단계적으로 집행된다. 당사 수주 전망치에는 2026년 중 4기
에 해당하는 장납기 기자재 발주 가능성을 반영했다. 장납기 기자재에는 원자로 및 
증기발생기가 포함된다. 협상 결과에 따라 추가적인 공급 범위(Scope) 확장도 가능
하다. 이에 더해 하반기 가스터빈 추가 수주 가능성이 높으며, SMR 역시 Nuscale 
향 수주를 연내 기대하고 있다. 수주 가이던스는 13.3조원으로 당사의 전망치와 괴리
가 있는데, 체코 두코바니 시공 수주 이후 가이던스 상향 조정 가능성이 열려있다.

대미 투자, 12차 전기본 – 주가 상승 트리거 풍부한 하반기 
투자의견 Buy, 적정주가는 145,000원에서 131,000원으로 -10% 하향한다. 장기 
마진율 조정에 따른 FCF 하락을 반영했으나 상승 여력 풍부하다. 주가 상승 트리거
로 1)수주, 2) 대미 투자, 3)12 차 전기본을 주목한다. 대미 투자는 빠르면 8월 말, 혹
은 9월 중 발표될 예정이며, 에너지 분야에서 이뤄질 전망이다. LNG, 가스 발전, 대
형원전, SMR 로 대표되는 에너지 사업 중 LNG 터미널을 제외한다면 동사가 참여가 
가능한 분야다. 당사는 대미 투자 유력 프로젝트로 SMR을 주목하는데, 만약 실현된
다면 대미 투자는 SMR 산업이 GW 급 양산을 시작하는 큰 분기점이 될 수 있다. 
이와 함께 Nuscale 향 기자재 발주도 연내 기대하고 있다. 또한 연말 전후 발표가 
예상되는 12차 전기본에서는 신규 원전 건설 계획 포함 가능성이 높다(3대 메가 프
로젝트 등 전력 수요 급증 때문). 이 역시 투자 심리를 개선시키는 요인이다.

Meritz Research  2026. 7. 28

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 131,000원 
현재주가 (7.27) 70,900원 
상승여력 84.8%

## Document 235: AI光通信需求强劲，Lumentum上季营收翻倍，本季指引更超预期，债务重组致超70亿巨亏｜财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI光通信需求强劲，Lumentum上季营收翻倍，本季指引更超预期，债务重组致超70亿巨亏｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-08-11
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

第四财季， Lumentum 营收同⽐增 109% 、⾮ GAAP ⼝径 ESP 增 267% ⾄ 3.23 美元，同⼝径
⽑利率略超 50% ，组件与系统业务营收均翻倍增⻓；⾮ GAAP ⼝径净利润同⽐增 415% ，但
GAAP ⼝径净亏损 72 亿美元、 EPS 为 -84.65 美元，源于⼀次性⾮现⾦债务清偿损失 78 亿美
元。第⼀财季，营收指引中值同⽐增 135% 、较市场预期⾼ 8% ， EPS 中值 282% 、较预期⾼
16% 。盘后股价先跌超 5% ，转涨超 5% 后⼜曾转跌。
摘要：
光通信概念股 Lumentum 公布的上⼀财季业绩和本季指引整体增⻓⽐华尔街预期的还迅猛，体现
了 AI 光通信需求的强劲推动势头。
美东时间 11 ⽇周⼆美股盘后， Lumentum 公布，截⾄ 2026 年 6 ⽉ 27 ⽇的公司第四财季，净营收同
⽐增⻓逾⼀倍⾄ 10.1 亿美元，较分析师预期⾼约 2% ；⾮ GAAP ⼝径下调整后每股收益（ EPS ）
同⽐⼤增 267% ⾄ 3.23 美元，较分析师预期⾼近 9% 。

利润率表现同样亮眼。第四财季⾮ GAAP ⼝径⽑利率达到 50.4% ，同⽐提升 1260 个基点；⾮
GAAP 经营利润率 36.6% ，同⽐提升 2160 个基点；调整后 EBITDA 为 4.064 亿美元，对应 EBITDA
利润率约 40% 。⾮ GAAP 净利润同⽐增⻓ 415% ⾄ 3.263 亿美元。
不过，因 Lumentum 将部分可转换票据转换为股权、产⽣⼀笔 78 亿美元的⼀次性⾮现⾦债务清偿
亏损，第四财季 GAAP ⼝径下录得净亏损为 72 亿美元。
Lumentum 本财季的指引明显强于预期。公司预计第⼀财季营收 12.3 亿⾄ 12.8 亿美元，指引区间
中值较市场预期⾼逾 8% ；调整后 EPS 预计 4.05 ⾄ 4.35 美元，指引中值较市场预期⾼逾 16% ；⾮
GAAP 经营利润率预计 39.5% ⾄ 40.5% 。这意味着公司不仅收⼊继续加速，利润率也有望迈向
40% 附近。
Lumentum CEO Michael Hurlston 表⽰，第四财季业绩展现了⼴泛的市场进展，关键增⻓动⼒

—— 如 OCS 解决⽅案和云模块业务也已开始贡献增量。 “ 对超⾼功率 CPO 激光器的需求增⻓、
ELS 模块的⾸批订单，以及我们在 NPO 领域⼴泛的业务布局，均是光学技术正开始渗透⾄机架
内连接领域的初期迹象，这将显著扩⼤我们的光学技术潜在市场规模（ TAM ）。 ”
财报公布后，周⼆收涨近 0.9% 的 Lumentum 的股价并未直线上⾏，⽽是剧烈震荡，盘后先曾转
跌并⼀度跌超 5% ，很快转涨后曾涨超 5% ，后⼜曾转跌。股价表现反映出市场⼀⽅⾯认可 AI 光通
信需求和强劲指引，另⼀⽅⾯也在消化 GAAP 巨亏、前期预期偏⾼和估值压⼒。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

分析认为，股价盘后波动本质上是三股⼒量在拉扯： AI 光通信需求与强指引带来的基本⾯上修，
GAAP 巨亏数字带来的短线冲击，以及⾼估值、⾼预期资产在财报季常⻅的获利了结。后续股价
能否稳定，市场⼤概率会继续盯住第⼀财季指引兑现情况、 40% 左右经营利润率能否维持，以
及 CPO 、 ELS 、 NPO 相关需求从早期订单⾛向规模化收⼊的速度。
第四财季盈利增⻓远超营收  EPS 同⽐增近三倍
Lumentum 第四财季净营收 10.1 亿美元，⾼于市场预期的约 9.9 亿美元，也远⾼于上年同期的
4.807 亿美元。
按同⽐⼝径计算，公司营收增⻓约 109% ，意味着这⼀季度收⼊规模已经超过去年同期的两倍。
虽然相较预期的超出幅度约 2% 左右，并不算 “ 爆炸性⼤超 ” ，但考虑到公司所在的 AI 光通信赛道
已被市场⾼度关注，能够继续超预期本⾝仍然具有信号意义。
第四财季调整后 EPS 为 3.23 美元，⾼于预期的 2.97 美元，同⽐增幅 267% 。这说明增⻓并⾮单纯
来⾃收⼊扩张，利润弹性更强。

这份财报最核⼼的变化，是 Lumentum 的盈利模型正在快速改善：⽑利率突破 50% ，经营利润率
升⾄ 36% 以上，说明⾼端光通信产品需求、规模效应和产品组合改善正在共同释放利润杠杆。
⼏个核⼼指标如下：
指标 Q4 实际 市场预期 上年同期 / 同⽐
净营收 10.1 亿美元 9.88 亿美元 4.807 亿美元
调整后 EPS 3.23 美元 2.97 美元 同⽐ +267%
调整后 EBITDA 4.064 亿美元 — —
⾮ GAAP ⽑利率 50.4% — 同⽐ +1260bp
⾮ GAAP 经营利润率 36.6% — 同⽐ +2160bp
⾮ GAAP 净利润 3.263 亿美元 — 同⽐ +415%

### Page 3

组件与系统业务双双超预期，系统业务同⽐增速更快
分业务看， Lumentum 第四财季组件与零部件业务营收 6.494 亿美元，⾼于分析师预期的 6.369 亿
美元，上年同期为 3.204 亿美元，同⽐增幅约 103% 。
系统业务营收 3.569 亿美元，同样⾼于市场预期的 3.504 亿美元，上年同期为 1.603 亿美元，同⽐
增幅约 123% 。
也就是说，两⼤业务都实现了翻倍增⻓：
业务 Q4 营收 市场预期 上年同期 同⽐增速
组件与零部件 6.494 亿美元 6.369 亿美元 3.204 亿美元 约 +103%
系统 3.569 亿美元 3.504 亿美元 1.603 亿美元 约 +123%
组件与零部件仍是公司收⼊基本盘，占总收⼊约六成以上；系统业务虽然规模较⼩，但同⽐增
速更快，显⽰需求扩张并⾮集中在单⼀产品线。
对光通信公司⽽⾔，这⼀点很重要。 AI 数据中⼼建设带来的不仅是单⼀器件需求，⽽是从激光
器、光器件到系统级解决⽅案的整体拉动。 Lumentum 本季度两个分部均超预期，强化了 “AI ⽹

络升级带动光学需求外溢 ” 的逻辑。
指引成最⼤亮点  本财季营收中值同⽐增 135%
如果说第四财季本⾝是 “ 稳稳超预期 ” ，那么下⼀财季指引才是这份财报真正点燃市场情绪的部
分。
Lumentum 预计第⼀财季：

### Page 4

营收 12.3 亿⾄ 12.8 亿美元，市场预期 11.6 亿美元；
调整后 EPS 为 4.05 ⾄ 4.35 美元，市场预期 3.61 美元；
⾮ GAAP 经营利润率为 39.5% ⾄ 40.5% 。
按指引中值计算，第⼀财季营收约 12.55 亿美元，相当于同⽐增⻓ 135% ，较第四财季环⽐增⻓
约 24% ；较市场预期⾼出约 8% 。调整后 EPS 中值约 4.20 美元，同⽐增⻓约 282% ，环⽐增⻓约
30% ，较预期⾼出约 16% 。
这意味着公司给出的不是 “ ⼩幅延续增⻓ ” ，⽽是收⼊和盈利继续明显上台阶。
尤其是⾮ GAAP 经营利润率指引达到 39.5% ⾄ 40.5% ，中值约 40% ，相⽐第四财季 36.6% 再提升
约 340 个基点。公司管理层称正在 “ ⽐计划提前超过⼀个季度达到⽬标模型 ” ，这句话背后对应的
是：收⼊规模扩⼤、产品组合改善和运营杠杆释放的速度都快于原先预期。
⽑利率突破 50% ， Lumentum 不只是 “ 卖得更多 ”
Lumentum 第四财季⾮ GAAP ⽑利率达到 50.4% ，同⽐提升 1260 个基点；⾮ GAAP 经营利润率

36.6% ，同⽐提升 2160 个基点。
这组数据说明， Lumentum 的业绩改善并⾮单纯依靠收⼊翻倍带来的规模扩张，⽽是利润质量同
步改善。
在 AI 光通信供应链中，市场最看重的往往不仅是订单增⻓，还包括产品是否具备较⾼技术壁垒、
是否能维持较⾼ ASP 和⽑利率。 Lumentum 本季度⽑利率升⾄ 50% 以上，经营利润率逼近 40% ，
对于市场理解其 AI 光学业务的盈利能⼒是重要⽀撑。
调整后 EBITDA 为 4.064 亿美元，占营收约 40.2% ；⾮ GAAP 净利润 3.263 亿美元，占营收约
32.3% 。在硬件链条公司中，这样的利润率⽔平意味着公司正在受益于较强的产品定价能⼒和经
营杠杆。
CPO 、 ELS 、 NPO ：光学需求正向机架内互联渗透
除了数字本⾝，管理层关于需求结构的表述同样值得关注。
Lumentum 提到，超⾼功率 CPO 激光器需求增加、 ELS 模块获得初始订单，以及 NPO 相关合作
范围扩⼤，是 “ 光学开始渗透机架内连接 ” 的早期信号，并显著扩⼤公司的光学可服务市场。

这⾥涉及⼏个关键技术⽅向：
CPO （ Co-Packaged Optics ，光电共封装）：将光学引擎更靠近交换芯⽚，降低⾼速电信号
传输损耗和功耗；
ELS （ External Laser Source ，外置激光源模块）：为 CPO 等架构提供外部激光源；
NPO （ Near-Packaged Optics ，近封装光学）：介于传统可插拔光模块和 CPO 之间的⼀类近
芯⽚光互联⽅案。
AI 集群规模扩⼤后，数据中⼼内部的带宽、功耗和延迟压⼒快速上升。传统电连接在更⾼速率、
更⾼密度场景下⾯临瓶颈，光互联开始从机架间、交换机间连接进⼀步向机架内、近芯⽚⽅向
渗透。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

1 条评论
这也是 Lumentum 这份财报最具想象⼒的部分：如果光学需求不再局限于传统数据中⼼互联，⽽
是进⼊机架内连接和近封装架构，公司潜在市场空间将被重新评估。
不过， Lumentum 也⽤了 “ 初期迹象 ” （ first signs ）这⼀表述，说明这些⽅向仍处于早期验证和订
单转化阶段。市场后续会重点关注这些 CPO 、 ELS 、 NPO 机会能否从初始订单和项⽬接洽，转
化为可持续的规模化收⼊。
GAAP 巨亏来⾃⼀次性⾮现⾦债务清偿损失
财报中最刺眼的数字，是 GAAP ⼝径下净亏损将近 72 亿美元、同⼝径 EPS 为 -84.65 美元。
Lumentum 说明，这主要包含⼀笔 78 亿美元的⼀次性⾮现⾦债务清偿损失。也就是说，这并⾮由

本季度经营恶化导致，⽽是债务清偿相关的会计处理造成的 GAAP 层⾯巨额亏损。
这也是为什么同⼀份财报⾥会同时出现两个看似⽭盾的信号： GAAP ⼝径下表⾯看是巨亏，⾮
GAAP 净利润却同⽐⼤增 415% ⾄ 3.263 亿美元。
对基本⾯投资者⽽⾔，更能反映当季经营状况的是⾮ GAAP 利润、⽑利率、经营利润率和下⼀季
指引。但对盘后交易和量化交易⽽⾔， GAAP 巨亏标题本⾝很容易引发第⼀轮抛压，尤其是在盘
后流动性较低的环境下。
换句话说， Lumentum 盘后最初⼀度跌超 5% ，很可能与 GAAP 巨亏、前期股价预期较⾼以及 “ 财
报兑现后卖出 ” 情绪有关，⽽不完全是对经营数据的否定。

## Document 240: AI服务器需求持续释放，工业富联上半年净利润同比增长96%｜财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI服务器需求持续释放，工业富联上半年净利润同比增长96%｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-08-11
Pages: 2

⼯业富联 2026 年上半年业绩⾼增，营收达 5578.61 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 54.63% ；净利润
237.40 亿元，近乎翻倍。 AI 需求为核⼼驱动⼒，云计算及 AI 服务器业务增⻓迅猛。盈利能
⼒与现⾦流同步提升， ROE 升⾄ 13.36% 。公司决定暂不分红，留存利润以⽀持 AI 及云计算
业务的持续扩张。
摘要：
⼯业富联交出⼀份⾼增⻓的中期成绩单。 2026 年上半年，公司实现营业收⼊ 5578.61 亿元，同⽐
增⻓ 54.63% ；归属于上市公司股东的净利润 237.40 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 95.99% ，接近翻倍。扣除⾮
经常性损益后的净利润为 229.84 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 96.99% ，业绩增⻓主要来⾃主营业务扩张。
从业务端看， AI 相关需求仍是公司业绩增⻓的核⼼驱动⼒。公司公告显⽰，上半年云计算业务营
业收⼊同⽐增⻓ 75.7% ， AI 相关业务贡献持续扩⼤。云服务商 AI 服务器营业收⼊同⽐增⻓ 2.3
倍， GPU AI 机柜出货量同⽐增⻓ 3.2 倍； ASIC AI 机柜出货量同⽐增⻓ 3 倍， ASIC CPU 服务器出

货量同⽐增⻓ 2.5 倍。
随着 GPU 及 ASIC 业务同步放量，⼯业富联 AI 服务器产品出货量⼤幅增⻓，全球 AI 服务器市场的
领先地位进⼀步巩固。

盈利能⼒同步提升。上半年，公司利润总额 278.17 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 98.37% ；加权平均净资产收
益率（ ROE ）由上年同期的 7.64% 升⾄ 13.36% ，提⾼ 5.72 个百分点；基本每股收益由 0.61 元升
⾄ 1.20 元，同⽐增⻓ 96.72% 。
现⾦流也明显改善。上半年，公司经营活动产⽣的现⾦流量净额为 73.91 亿元，同⽐增⻓
425.32% ，经营现⾦流增幅显著⾼于营收和净利润增速。
从盈利结构看，扣⾮净利润达到归⺟净利润的 96.8% ，两者仅相差约 7.56 亿元，显⽰⾮经常性损
益对整体利润贡献有限。
暂不分红，利润留存⽀持扩张
分红⽅⾯，公司董事会决议显⽰， 2026 年上半年不派发现⾦红利、不送红股，也不进⾏资本公

积⾦转增股本。
在净利润同⽐接近翻倍的情况下，公司选择留存利润，意味着资⾦将更多⽤于业务扩张和再投
资。随着 AI 服务器、云计算等业务持续增⻓，公司仍处于较强的资本投⼊阶段。
整体来看，⼯业富联上半年呈现出营收⾼速增⻓、利润增速更快、 ROE 提升以及经营现⾦流改
善的特征， AI 服务器等⾼增⻓业务对公司业绩的拉动仍在持续。

1WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUSTRALIA &
NEW ZEALAND
WEEKLY
Week beginning 10 August 2026
In this week’s edition:
Economic Insight: Buckle up for some short-term housing turbulence.
The Week That Was: Consumer shows resilience amid housing gloom.
Focus on New Zealand: Plenty of slack in the labour market.

For the week ahead:
Australia: RBA policy decision, Governor Bullock testifying before House of Representatives, dwelling
finance approvals, business survey.
New Zealand: inflation expectations, house prices and sales, net migration, manufacturing PMI.
China: current account balance.
Euro Area: industrial production, trade balance, investor confidence.
United Kingdom: Q2 GDP.
United States: CPI, PPI, retail sales, UoM consumer sentiment, NFIB small business optimism, home sales.

Analysis and forecasts for this week’s key releases.
Information contained in this report current as at 7 August 2026.

### Page 2

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
• Tight monetary policy plus tax changes add up to a powerful
combination weighing on the housing market. Credit remains
readily available, but demand is weak. The various indicators
point to a weaker near-term outlook for prices than the

base-case projections we published in late June.
• The RBA is expected to keep the cash rate on hold at its
August meeting. This is an outworking of the better inflation
outlook; the RBA was never likely to overreact to the fourth
housing downturn in a decade. Targeting housing prices is
not part of the central bank’s remit, and most of the softness
will be seen as normal policy transmission.
• Looking beyond this year, a lower path for interest rates

and earlier timing of subsequent rate cuts mean that any
housing market downturn is likely to be relatively short-
lived – an “air pocket”, not a crash.
Since the Federal Budget in May, alarmist predictions about
the housing market consequences of the announced tax
changes have become commonplace. Recent interest rate
rises, together with these changes, are clearly weighing on
housing turnover and prices, leaving both buyers and sellers
more uncertain.

As the pandemic experience made clear, when fiscal and
monetary policy work together, they make for a powerful
combination. The current situation is an example: a tightening
of tax policy affecting housing coinciding with tight monetary
policy. Given housing is a notably interest-sensitive sector,
the conditions are therefore in place for a weaker housing
market. Importantly, though, this is not the same kind of policy
shock as the prudential policy changes and Royal Commission

response of the 2010s. Credit supply is not directly affected
and remains readily available.
There are, however, signs that things are turning out a bit
weaker than our projections , released in late June. Housing
price growth has turned down across all cities, with outright
declines for most in the latest month; while we expected
prices to move lower, the momentum is running a little ahead
of our projections .
How people interpret this weakness is an open question.

In recent times, when prices have been expected to fall,
consumers have seen this as meaning it is a less bad time to buy
a home. This contrasts with the usual historical pattern when
strong price growth was seen as heralding a good time to buy.
Buckle up for some short-term housing turbulence
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
A slightly-less-bad view of affordability as prices decline
is not currently translating to buyer interest, though. The

uncertainty around the effects of the tax changes could be
inducing some negativity, or at least some divergence of views
across the household sector, especially between investors and
prospective owner-occupiers. Timely industry data show home
loan credit enquiries have declined to levels last seen in late
2022 and are falling at a monthly pace of around 4%. This is
broadly consistent with our forecast for turnover to decline by
around 20% this year.

Indicators of conditions in the physical market are also weaker.
Auction clearance rates have fallen to previous cycle lows in
Sydney and Melbourne. Turnover has declined sharply in most
jurisdictions, and while new listings are also starting to move
lower, they are falling more slowly than sales. As a result, stock
on market is rising relative to the flow of sales and turning
established housing into more of a “buyer’s market”. That said,
the data also show notable divergences across cities, highlighting

that local market conditions matter, and often differ materially.
While things seem a bit softer than we earlier projected,
consider the starting point. In all major cities other than
Melbourne, housing prices had run up considerably in recent
years. A moderate decline would still leave prices notably
higher than a few years ago. As the RBA has pointed out
recently, the number of people in negative equity (home value
below mortgage balance) is very low, and even a moderate

decline in prices will not add much to this figure. While
unemployment is forecast to drift up from here, this is mostly a
story of job growth failing to keep pace with strong population
growth and rising participation, not outright job losses. “Fire
sales” by distressed borrowers are therefore unlikely to add
downward pressure to housing prices while job prospects for
mortgagors remain reasonable.
Meanwhile, the grandfathering of tax changes means that

current owners of investment properties have little incentive
to sell, while owner-occupiers are also likely to want to stand
back and avoid catching the “falling knife”. A period of low
“The housing market might be entering
a short-term ‘air pocket’ but greater
turbulence does not imply a crash. "

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
turnover is likely; this kind of thin market can result in prices
being volatile, but it typically lacks the wave of distressed
selling that is needed to induce large, self-reinforcing
downward price spirals.
RBA will not take fright
The question then becomes how the RBA will respond. Will

it see a softer housing market as simply the normal channel of
policy transmission, or an additional shock with the potential
for a more alarming over-correction? Some of the more dovish
perspectives we have heard from customers tilt to the “over-
correction” story, but in our view, the RBA was never likely to
react strongly to the fourth housing downturn in a decade.
The RBA’s mandate relates to inflation and full employment;
housing prices are not a policy objective – and in any case, it

is not obvious what such an objective would entail. Would
a financial stability lens favour gently rising prices to avoid
negative equity and loan distress? Or would an affordability
lens favour declining prices? There are good reasons why
Parliament has not charged the RBA with targeting housing
prices, and good reasons why the housing element in the
RBNZ’s mandate was dropped after a few years.
As RBA officials have been at pains to point out, the Bank

does not target housing prices but instead glean information
from them. Weak prices tend to induce weak turnover, which
contributes to softer economic activity. Lower housing prices
can over time induce negative wealth effects on consumption
– though any period of decline must be set against earlier
run-ups. How the housing market is performing tells the RBA
something about how monetary policy is playing through the
economy. Some softness is expected when interest rates are

high; the question is whether that softness is greater or less
than expected. The experience of 2018–19 shows that soft
housing markets and weak growth in household incomes can
weigh on consumption – and so inflation – more than expected.
But recall that was when inflation and interest rates were
already subdued,  fiscal policy was contractionary  and there
were no offsetting boosts to demand from infrastructure, data
centres, or other forms of investment.

We also note that, since our last published release of housing
price forecasts in the late-June Housing Pulse, we have taken
out the two RBA rate hikes we previously expected and brought
forward the timing of the subsequent rate cuts from February
2028 to August 2027. A period of stability for rates in the near
term might be enough to temper the downside risks in housing
or even allow for some stabilisation. Whether this plays out will
become clearer as we head into the spring selling season.

In addition, earlier rate cuts mean the outlook for 2027 is
a bit brighter than before, further supporting the idea that
weakness this year is an “air pocket”, not a lasting downturn.
We will formally revise our housing price forecasts in next
week’s Market Outlook, after the RBA decision. Directionally,
though, the story is near-term weakness in 2026, not a
downturn stretching into 2027.

### Page 4

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: consumer shows resilience amid housing gloom
However, the employment index fell 3.8pts to 47.4, an outright
contractionary read.
While a month behind the ISMs, the latest JOLTS job report
points to less demand for labour across a number of key

services sub-sectors including healthcare (-147k to 1,347k),
education (-133k to 1,475k), professional & business services
(-71k to 1,304k), and accommodation & food services (-68k to
684k). The only meaningful offset came from transportation &
warehousing, which rose 119k to 1,369k reflecting a seasonal
pattern. That said, the current job opening rate for the economy
overall is consistent with labour demand and supply remaining
broadly in balance as opposed to outright declines. Tonight sees

the eagerly awaited release of the July employment report.
In China, the RatingDog manufacturing PMI fell 0.8pts to
50.9 in July, its weakest reading in four months. The headline
decline was concentrated in production and new orders.
Consistent with a soft domestic economy, price pressures
eased further, input price inflation slowing to a six-month low
and output price growth remaining broadly flat. The services
PMI experienced a sharper fall to 50.4 in July, its lowest

reading since September 2024. The July decline was primarily
driven by weaker growth in domestic new orders. Employment
continued to expand, albeit at a slower pace, while business
sentiment fell to its lowest level since early 2020. Clearly
there is need for pro-active stimulus in scale through the
second half of the year.
Finally, in the Middle East, a deal between Iran and Oman over
transit through the Strait of Hormuz is reportedly in the final

stages of drafting. If implemented, it will allow safe passage
for commercial ships. Iran has said, however, that US and
Israeli-linked vessels will not be allowed to transit while the
US’ blockade remains in effect. Whether this stance causes
an issue for the implementation of this deal and/or any further
progress towards a lasting peace is a question for another day.
Market participants continue to take a glass half full view, with
Brent oil having traded near USD80 most of this week.

In Australia, the week kicked off with a gloomy update
on the housing market. Cotality’s nationwide home value
index dropped another 0.7% in July, following declines of
0.5% and 0.7% in May and June respectively, leaving prices
1.6% below their March peak. The scale of the correction is
broadening across the capitals, with momentum in Brisbane
and Adelaide abruptly shifting from gains to losses, while
prices in Sydney and Melbourne fall more than 1% per

month. The combination of restrictive monetary policy, weak
homebuyer sentiment following the Budget and general
economic uncertainty makes for a challenging backdrop.
Shifting focus to the consumer, recent readings on household
spending point to a degree of resilience. Nominal household
spending rounded out the quarter with a 0.8% increase in June,
taking the Q2 gain to 1.3%. Just under half of that increase
was driven by higher prices (0.6%), with higher volumes

(0.7%) accounting for the remainder of the gain – implying
slightly higher inflation and slightly lower real spending versus
Q1. Gains across household contents, recreation & culture and
hospitality point to the potential for improved discretionary
spending, however.
Our Q2 Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel  – a comprehensive
update on income, spending and saving flows across the
country – adds useful context. It shows that income growth
has trailed spending growth over the past year, prompting

households to draw down on savings. However, this
drawdown had a relatively robust starting point given the
median consumer had rebuilt savings buffers to around 2022
levels prior to the post-pandemic tightening cycle. While
conditions remain uneven across the mortgage belt, the
aggregate drawdown this year has been relatively modest,
suggesting households retain a degree of financial resilience.
Before moving offshore, a final note on trade. June’s goods

trade data surprised to the upside, recording a surplus of
$1.9bn following May’s deficit of $2.4bn. Gold exports was
the primary driver of monthly volatility, although a solid
performance from iron ore also contributed to export earnings.
For Q2 as a whole, goods trade appears set to detract around
½ppt from GDP growth, most notably due to large increases in
fuel and EV imports.
Over in the US, the manufacturing PMI rose 2.3pts to 55.6, its
highest level since May 2022, as production jumped 6.3pts to

58.5 and new orders remained firmly in expansionary territory
at 56.7. Employment also returned to expansion for the first
time in nearly three years. For the services sector, the headline
PMI was broadly unchanged at 54.1, but the detail was mixed.
New orders lifted 2.1pts to 57.2 and business activity 3.7pts.
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
Plenty of slack in the labour market
The economy appears to have weathered the Iran war fairly
well but has not emerged totally unscathed. Labour market
data this week confirms significant excess capacity remains
which is helping to restrain inflation pressures. Nevertheless,

inflation remains too high, and wage pressures appear sticky,
raising questions on how easy it will be to bring inflation
back to 2%. The RBNZ will likely need to do more than they
currently say to achieve that. Markets seem to agree with
that assessment even though the sequencing of OCR hikes
beyond September seems murky.
It’s been a rougher than ideal five months for the economy.
Economic recovery had taken hold from the September quarter
of 2025 and delivered three above-trend quarters (averaged)

until the Iran war stepped in and interrupted progress. The
war saw a reduction and increased volatility in consumer
and business sentiment and consumer spending. Increased
uncertainty associated with the energy supply situation
reasonably saw consumers and businesses retreat into their
shells while the shooting was going on.
We can now see that GDP Nowcasts and high frequency activity
indicators are consistent with a flat to negative June quarter 2026

growth outcome. Nevertheless, this is a better performance
than was feared early in the war when the risks of very high fuel
costs and potential supply disruptions were in prospect. Early
indicators for September quarter growth look noticeably stronger
than the June quarter and suggest a return to growth.
Whether that Q3 growth is above trend remains to be seen.
Uncertainty remains high as we go into the sixth month of the
Iran war. The Strait of Hormuz remains effectively closed and

the entry of the Yemen Houthis into the fray is interrupting
alternative Red Sea supply routes. Deals are purportedly on
the table but at the time of writing the shooting continues and
oil prices are moving up into the mid USD80s again. Recent
strength in consumer and business confidence seems fragile
and its sustainability will be heavily dependent on whether
some kind of Middle East stability can be found. And as
noted, prospects there remain very murky. The Government

announced this week an extension to the fuel relief package
that was initially implemented in March to assist lower income
households. Technically, the threshold to withdraw the scheme
was close to being met in the last week. But rising fuel costs
and high uncertainty meant it was pragmatic for the government
to maintain the support for now.
This week’s data confirmed that significant slack remains in the
labour market. The key signal came from the unemployment rate

which rose more than expected to a new cycle high of 5.6%. The
RBNZ will likely put the most weight on this observation when
determining how much downward pressure excess capacity is
exerting on inflation at its still very elevated level.
Interestingly though, the details of the labour market report
were messy, and not uniformly consistent with a weak economy
view. The official measure of household employment growth
and labour force participation surpassed expectations – albeit

possibly exaggerated by survey sample error given what we
see in other employment measures – continuing a trend seen for
a few quarters now. At face value, slack was created through
a rise in labour supply that stronger than expected labour
Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist NZ

### Page 6

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
6
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
demand could not overcome. That’s not fundamentally a weak
economic story. Employment growth is firmly positive in a 0.4-
1.2% y/y range which is consistent with the lift in GDP growth
seen since the middle of last year.
We see significant diversity in labour market performance around

the country. The South Island, which is more heavily driven
by the strong agricultural and tourism sectors, saw a falling
unemployment rate which is close to the 10-year average. At
the same time the more heavily service sector dominated North
Island urban centres were weak and unemployment is almost
2 standard deviations above the 10-year average. Population
growth trends likely reflect these divergent performances.
Interestingly, wage growth slightly surpassed expectations

and looks sticky at 3.1% on an unadjusted Labour Cost Index
(LCI) basis. The headline productivity-adjusted LCI growth
rate of 2% – less than half of its pandemic peak – is consistent
with achieving 2% inflation over the medium term, but as yet
labour market slack seems not to have led to sub-2% wage
growth. This raises questions on the future direction of wage
growth given elevated inflation expectations and reduced
labour market slack as the economy further recovers. Firms

report a rising proportion of wage demands that reflect
cost of living pressures. This tells us the public has noticed
elevated inflation and sees it as sufficiently persistent to
warrant including in the wage bargaining process. This could
be a headwind for the RBNZ to overcome if it hopes to return
inflation to 2% in any reasonable timeframe.
This week the RBNZ’s Governor commented to the press on
policy and the economy. She understandably reiterated her

goal of returning inflation to 2%. However, the strategy to
achieve this is unclear as the policy strategy at this point is
limited to “taking away a little bit of the stimulus”. The RBNZ’s
forecasts are for the OCR to return to their view of long-term
neutral levels as opposed to restrictive levels. Hence, it’s
unclear how this strategy will push core inflation down from
the current 2.5-3% level to 2% as the economy recovers.
Disinflation may or may not happen within a reasonable

timeframe and could easily be knocked off course by future
inflation shocks (for example El Niño driven food price risks).
In all likelihood, more work will be required in time. Historically
this has been the behaviour of the RBNZ. Early in the cycle,
forecasts often indicate a modest tightening cycle that is scaled
up as the cycle progresses. Hence, we remain comfortable with
the idea the OCR reaches 4% sometime in 2027.
In the immediate period though, the observation of increased

labour market slack asks questions of the cadence of OCR
increases to end 2026. A tightening in September remains
a strong possibility as we currently forecast. The elevated
headline inflation outlook also strongly points to a September
OCR increase. However, the case for an October hike remains
unclear given the unemployment rate has lifted by more than
the RBNZ forecast in May. The MPC can reach a year end OCR
of 3% without an October move. We really don’t have enough

clarity on the MPC’s strategy to be sure how meetings beyond
September will progress.
There has been a shift away from providing forward guidance
in central banks globally. Both the US and Australian central
bank governors have shied away from such guidance recently.
It’s important though for the policy reaction function and
strategy to be clear. Doubts have emerged in the US recently
about the Federal Reserve’s reaction function and strategy to
return inflation to 2%. These doubts have impacted on long

term interest rates and some measures of policy credibility. We
think it will be important that the RBNZ doesn’t also slip into
the same trap. Hence at some point the RBNZ should clarify
how exactly inflation will be returned to target. Hope is not a
strategy, but raising the OCR to restrictive levels is a credible
approach. The RBNZ will need ultimately to move away from
the “removing stimulus” line and be upfront on the path ahead
for the OCR through 2027. Perhaps we will hear more in the

September Monetary Policy Statement.
Lastly, the election campaign period is now upon us, and the
race looks very close. While for much of the year the polls
have pointed to a right-leaning coalition being returned to
power with NZ First being the kingmaker, the situation is
now changing. The Opportunity Party is gaining ground and
increasingly looks likely to breach the key 5% threshold. These
developments have shifted the calculus and made a left-

leaning coalition more likely and has positioned Opportunity
as the deciding factor. Hence political uncertainty in on the rise
and the future policy mix is in flux. Watch this space!

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: RBA Policy Decision (%)
Aug 11, Last: 4.35, Westpac f/c: 4.35
Market f/c: 4.35, Range: 4.35 to 4.35
Westpac expects the RBA Monetary Policy Board (MPB) will
keep the cash rate unchanged at 4.35% at its August meeting.
The Q2 CPI came in below our expectations on both a headline

and trimmed mean basis. We had highlighted the risks in our
preview and in the event, only the downside risks materialised.
This is welcome news – the substantial pass-through of higher
energy costs seen in the early phase of the Middle East conflict
has not been followed up in recent months.
The MPB will likely retain a hawkish posture as it assess the
data flow over the coming months. Should the data behave
as expected, the MPB can gain confidence that inflation is

returning to target under existing policy settings.
AUS: Q2 Housing Finance Approvals (%qtr)
Aug 14, Last: –3.8, Westpac f/c: –6.0
Market f/c: –4.0, Range: –7.0 to –1.0
The total value of housing finance approvals fell –3.8% in
Q1, the largest drop in three years, annual growth slowing
to 18.5%yr. The fall was volume-led and slightly more
pronounced for owner-occupier than for investors.
We expect a more pronounced 6% decline in Q2. Interest
rate rises in March and May, an uncertain near-term economic

outlook, and the start of a sharp pull back in investor activity
following housing-related tax policy changes announced in the
Federal Budget are all at play. Loan enquiries and turnover are
down materially, with price declines pointing to lower average
loan values. It's unclear how much of the move is captured in
Q2 or Q3. We expect the Q2 mix to show investor approvals
down 9.7%qtr and owner occupier approvals down 3.5%qtr.
NZ: Jul REINZ House Sales & Prices (%ann)

Aug 13, Sales, Last: –2.9; Prices, Last: –0.8
The housing market softened further in June. House sales were
down around 1% in seasonally adjusted terms, adding to a
cumulative 9% fall since the start of the year. The REINZ house
price index fell by 0.3% for the month and was down 0.8%
on a year ago. Fixed-term mortgage rates have risen since the
start of this year, in anticipation of the next RBNZ tightening
cycle (which is now in motion).
We expect that higher interest rates will continue to weigh

on the housing market, leaning against any improvement in
incomes as the economy gets back on its feet.

### Page 8

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
NZ: Q3 RBNZ Survey of Inflation Expectations (%ann)
Aug 13, Inflation Two Years Ahead, Last: 2.53
Inflation expectations are a key focus for the RBNZ right
now, with concerns that the oil-related rise in inflation will
result in a broader and more enduring lift in pricing behaviour.

Expectations already took a step higher in the Q2 survey.
Since then, we’ve seen large swings in oil prices, while overall
inflation has risen back up to 4.1%.
We’re expecting that the Q3 survey will show another rise in
inflation expectations at the closely watched one- and two-
year ahead horizons. However, expectations at longer horizons
(five and ten years ahead) are likely to be more stable given the
start of the RBNZ’s tightening cycle.
US: Jul CPI (%mth)

Aug 12, Last: –0.4, Market f/c: 0.1, Westpac f/c: 0.2
June’s headline outcome benefitted materially from
the month’s sharp drop in energy prices as the US/Iran
memorandum came into effect. Other components of the
inflation basket were benign, with core prices unchanged.
July’s print will be boosted by the recommencement of strikes
by both Iran and the US, with Brent oil surging back through
USD100 late in the month. The remainder of the basket is

expected to hold to the modest trend of the first half, with
both core and headline inflation expected to print at 0.2%.
In terms of the risks, the market will be watching closely
for any signs of secondary effects, particularly in transport
services. If these are seen, expect market participants to price
in a high chance of a hike by the FOMC in September.

## Document 250: Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, July (2026-08-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, July (2026-08-12).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#businessconditions#confidence #aud #australia
Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, July
By Luka Belobrajdic
Economist, Westpac
14:26 August 11 2026 Share
A re-escalation of the conflict in the Middle East sees the recovery in business confidence
stall, while business conditions improve modestly. Confidence unchanged at -6 / Conditions
+1pt to 4.

### Page 2

The July NAB Business Survey was in the field from 21 to 31 July, capturing businesses’ reaction to the
breakdown in the US-Iran Memorandum of Understanding and the brief spike in Brent oil prices above
US$100/bbl. Business confidence held steady at -6 in July, following a downwardly revised reading of
-6 in June (-1pt), marking the first month since the onset of the conflict in which confidence failed to
improve. While well above the low recorded in March, and showing a marked improvement on a three-

month average basis, confidence remains deeply pessimistic, sitting 11pts below its long-run average
and remaining in the bottom 10% of outcomes recorded since 1997.
The stagnation in business confidence likely partly reflects a reacceleration in cost pressures during
the month. Final product price growth nearly doubled, rising 0.6ppt to 1.1%qtr, suggesting firms are
seeking to rebuild margins following several months of subdued profitability outcomes. While still below

the 1.8%qtr peak recorded at the height of the Middle East conflict in April, the result marks a renewed
move above its long-run average (+0.6ppt). Meanwhile, input costs keep ramping up. Labour cost
growth accelerated, rising 0.5ppt to 2.2%qtr, the strongest outcome since May 2024 and 1.0ppt above
its long-run average. The increase likely reflects the full impact of the 4.75% increase in award wages.
And purchase cost growth increased 0.2ppt to 2.3%qtr, remaining 1.1ppt above its long-run average.

The headline business conditions index improved modestly in July, rising 1pt to 4 after holding at 3 over
the previous three months. Despite the improvement, conditions remain 3pts below their long-run
average and well below the peak of 11 recorded in October last year. On balance, the result suggests
businesses remain resilient despite the prevailing uncertainty.

### Page 3

Across the major sub-components, employment drove the majority of improvement in conditions, rising
2pts to 3 and more than reversing last month’s decline. The result leaves the sub-index 1pt above its
long-run average. The lift in hiring intentions comes despite a sharp increase in labour costs,
suggesting firms are, for now, looking through the higher wage costs. Profitability rose 1pt to 1 after
holding around 0 since March, indicating some improvement in margins, though the sub-index remains

4pts below its long-run average. Trading conditions were unchanged at 8 for a fourth consecutive
month, consistent with household spending data showing spending has remained resilient despite
ongoing headwinds facing consumers.
Capacity utilisation rose sharply in July, increasing 1.0ppt to 83.0%, a level last seen around the turn of
the year. The result sits 1.6ppt above its long-run average and marks the first increase in three-month
average terms since November last year. While too late to influence today’s RBA decision, the data

points to an upside risk to the underlying inflationary pressures in the economy. Capital expenditure
also rose modestly, increasing 1pt (rounded) to 3. However, this did little to unwind the sharp decline
recorded in June, leaving the sub-index 4pts below its long-run average.
Among other indicators, forward orders fell 3pts to -3, highlighting fragility of demand amid ongoing
uncertainty. Inventories declined by 1pt to 4, consistent with recently softer forward orders.

Charts of the week: Earnings likely to trump geopolitics

Source: Bloomberg, LSEG I/B/E/S, Standard Chartered

Editorial 
Geopolitics, policy and earnings 
Strategy summary:  The Houthis’ blockade of the Red Sea 
following the resumption of US -Iran hostilities has driven a 
sharp rebound in energy prices and bond yields. We expect US 
electoral constraints and Iranian restraint to eventually limit 
escalation in the conflict. Agai nst this backdrop, central banks 
are likely to hold rates this month as they assess the impact of 
any temporary spike in energy prices on the inflation outlook.  
Besides the spike in bond yields, equity markets will have to 
contend with lofty earnings expectations as the reporting 
season picks up. We expect strong AI investment -driven 
earnings beats, especially in the US and Asia, to drive upgrades 
to profit estimates. This should support continued equity market 
outperformance in the coming months. In fixed income, 
inflation-protected bonds appear particularly attractive, 
especially in the US, where the inflation -adjusted yield has 
surged above 2.4%, close to the highest since 2008.  
Mideast conflict limited by US electoral constraints, Iran 
restraint: The re-escalation of tensions, with Yemen’s Houthis 
joining the conflict, has driven European gas prices back to 
March peaks, but US gasoline prices remain well below the 
latest peaks. Notably, Iran has avoided attacking Saudi Arabia 
and UAE in the latest  escalation, signalling restraint. While 
there is a window for further near-term escalation, any rebound 
in US gasoline prices closer to May’s peaks in the run -up to 
November’s mid -term elections is likely to put pressure on 
President Trump to resume negot iations. Thus, we expect US 
crude oil (WTI) to trade largely in the USD 70 -90/bbl range in 
the next few months, limiting the economic impact.    
Central banks to hold rates this month.  We expect major 
central banks to hold rates this month as they assess the 
inflationary impact of the latest Middle East escalation. This was 
apparent from this week’s ECB decision to hold its deposit rate 
at 2.25%, while it kept the option open for another rate hike later 
this year. Meanwhile, the softer -than-expected US inflation 
report for June confirmed our view that inflation peaked in Q2. 
We expect the Fed to stay on hold this year as long as gasoline 
prices remain subdued, given slowing wage growth and cooling 
shelter and services inflation. As such, the 2-5-year segment of 
the bond yield curve looks increasingly attractive, especially if 
rate expectations peak with oil prices in the next few weeks.  
ECB, BoJ under rising pressure to hike. The ECB, being an 
inflation-targeting central bank, is under growing pressure to 
hike rates in the coming months to counter any energy -price 
driven inflation if European natural gas prices stay elevated. We 
raise the probability of another 25bps rate hike later this year, 
with the timing dependent on the status of the US -Iran conflict 
and the pass-through of energy prices onto inflation and wage 
growth. The BoE is likely to be constrained from hiking rates, 
though, due to weakening domestic growth outlook. Meanwhile, 
the BoJ remains behind the curve as a weaker currency pours 
fuel on domestic wage -driven inflation. There is rising chance 
the BoJ hikes twice this year, if only to support the JPY. 
Equities under rates, earnings scanner. Equities slumped on 
Thursday weighed by a Big Tech earnings report, surging crude 
oil prices (WTI crossed USD 90/bbl and Brent rose above USD 
100/bbl) and the US 10 -year government bond yield rising 
above 4.7%. The 10-year yield has gained almost 35bps since 
29 June. We believe a 25bps rise in bond yields, which serves 
as the discount rate for valuing equities, can impact global 
equities by around 3-4%, so investors should remain disciplined 
as rate expectations shift. However, projected earnings growth 
of 15 -20% in 2026 an d 2027 should provide a meaningful 
cushion against rate-driven valuation pressure. 
US earnings key support for equity outperformance . Big 
Tech is likely to show strong results and clearer AI monetisation 
in Q2. The tech sector has driven over half of global equity EPS 
upgrades since end-February, while AI adoption is still early in 
a multi-year cycle. We remain overweight US technology and 
communication services, and favour regions with stronger 
earnings visibility, particularly the US and Asia ex-Japan.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 24 July 2026

Top client questions 
  What do you expect from US Big Tech earnings next week? 
Our view: We expect US Big Tech companies to deliver solid Q2 
earnings while demonstrating further evidence  of AI 
monetisation. We are Overweight the US technology and 
communication services sectors. 
Rationale: The US technology sector continues to be a primary 
driver of S&P500 earnings, with the highest projected earnings 
growth over the next 12 months. It is expected to see earnings 
growth of 65.6% y/y in Q2 and 57% in 2026, driven by AI demand. 
Besides strong earnings growth, we expect US Big Tech companies 
to demonstrate that elevated AI capex is translating into stronger 
monetisation. US Census Bureau data indicates that AI adoption 
has already reached 20 -25% across US industries. This rapid 
uptake signals strong enterprise acceptance and gives us greater 
confidence that AI usage remains in the early stages of a multi-year 
growth cycle. The emergence of cost-effective and high-performing 
Chinese AI models has raised some concerns about AI 
commoditisation, which  can reduce the returns on AI capex. 
However, we believe that as AI resources become more efficient to 
use, total consumption will rise (Jevons paradox). To gain exposure, 
we are Overweight the US technology and communication services 
sectors, with the latter being dominated by large internet platforms. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist 
 What is your Q2 earnings outlook for the Japan and Europe 
financial sectors? 
Our view: We expect a constructive earnings season for Japan 
and Europe ex-UK banks and remain Overweight both sectors. 
Rationale: The earnings backdrop for Japan banks is supported by 
the BoJ’s two 25bps rate hikes in December 2025 and Jun e 2026, 
which are feeding through via  lagged loan repricing  and 
expanding domestic net interest margins . Notably, the 
regulatory easing  of Japan banks’ exposure limits  for individual 
corporate groups grants megabanks greater flexibility to underwrite 
large-scale M&A and AI data centre transactions. While the near -
term earnings benefit is incremental, the medium -term implications 
are more meaningful, with greater flexibility to support loan growth.  
Europe banks  are also enter ing the  Q2 earnings season with a 
constructive set of catalysts. Net interest income is benefiting from a 
higher-for-longer rate path, and fee income is supported by resilient 
investment banking activity and a stabilising wealth management 
segment. Meanwhile, credit quality remains robust , with the 
European Banking Authority's June 2026 Risk Assessment showing 
non-performing loan ratios sitting at historic lows. Sustained capital 
distributions via dividends and share buybacks continue to be a key 
catalyst and represent a compelling risk-adjusted return opportunity. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst

The technology sector is expected to continue 
leading the US market in earnings growth over the 
next 12 months

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 24 July 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What might be the implications of next week’s  Fed, BoJ 
and BoE policy meetings for bond and FX markets? 
Our view: We expect the Fed, BoJ and BoE to keep policy rates 
unchanged. Guidance will be key: i) a neutral Fed should limit 
further gains in US bond yields and USD, ii) BoJ is expected to 
keep the door open to further policy normalisation; and iii) the 
BoE is likely to retain a cautious inflation bias. 
Rationale: We expect the Fed to hold rates at 3.5-3.75%. Softer 
June US consumer inflation  supports a hold, although sustained 
strength in WTI oil prices could reinforce upside inflation risks. 
A neutral message w ill likely cap short -term gains in government 
bond yields and reduce support for the USD, while renewed 
emphasis on inflation risks would lift yields elevated. The USD Index 
(DXY) is likely to remain rangebound between 100.3 and 102.  
The BoJ is likely to hold its policy rate at 1.00% next week , but 
the weak JPY and imported inflation should preserve the possibility 
of further normalisation. Reports suggest some policymakers see 
scope for faster policy normalisation if JPY weakness, higher fuel 
costs and quicker cost pass -through push inflation above 
projections. Combined with our expectation that the Fed  will remain 
on hold, this should eventually allow USD/JPY to ease. Technically, 
we believe the USD/JPY correction is likely to be gradual rather than 
immediate. FX intervention and higher pension fund allocations 
to domestic assets may slow JPY depreciation, but a sustained 
reversal will require a narrower US-Japan interest rate differential. A 
sustained move below 160 will bring the 155-158 range into focus. 
We expect another hold by BoE on 30 July, following June’s 7-2 vote 
to hold rates at 3.75%. Persistent energy-driven inflation could keep 
UK Gilt yields elevated and support GBP, although weak activity and 
fiscal uncertainty may limit gains. GBP/USD is likely to stay 
rangebound between 1.3200 and 1.3500.   
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
 Will the PBoC implement monetary easing measures , 
given China’s weak Q2 GDP? Will this affect the CNH outlook? 
Our view: A reserve requirement ratio (RRR) cut remains 
possible, but any resulting CNH depreciation should be limited.  
Rationale: China’s economy grew 4.7% in H1, in line with the 
government’s annual growth target, although Q2 GDP moderated to 
4.3% y/y from 5.0%  in Q1 . Industrial production and exports 
remained resilient . Overall macroeconomic conditions do not 
suggest a urgent need for broad-based monetary easing. An RRR 
cut could weigh on the CNH initially by reinforcing concerns over 
wider policy divergence with the US. However, such a cut primarily 
releases banking system liquidity and does not necessarily signal 
a shift towards aggressive monetary easing. We therefore expect 
any CNH weakness to remain  measured, supported by China’s 
external balance and policymakers’ preference for exchange -rate 
stability. USD/CNH has near term support at 6.75. 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

between 100.3 and 102

## Document 330: Nemotron 3.5才落地已瞄准万亿参数！英伟达被曝正开发全球顶尖开源模型

Source PDF: all report pdf/Nemotron 3.5才落地已瞄准万亿参数！英伟达被曝正开发全球顶尖开源模型.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-08-11
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

据报道，英伟达正全⼒研发⾄少达 1 万亿参数的开源模型 Nemotron 4 ，旨在降低对 OpenAI
等头部客⼾及云巨头的依赖。公司不仅通过回租服务器将云服务算⼒承诺⼤幅增⾄ 280 亿美
元，还组建了包括 Mistral 、 Cursor 等在内的 “Nemotron 联盟 ” 共建⽣态。英伟达周⼆发布
300 亿参数、 30 亿活跃参数的模型 Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 及开源模型路由库 NeMo
Switchyard ，分别主打⾼效智能体执⾏与多模型智能调度。戴尔宣布扩⼤与英伟达合作，
将英伟达官宣的这两项技术纳⼊ Deskside Agentic AI ，推动智能体向企业本地部署延伸。
摘要：
英伟达正在加速向开源 AI 模型的前沿竞争发起冲击，试图以此扩⼤ GPU 需求 —— 尽管这⼀策略
同时将其推⼊与⾃⾝客⼾和投资对象正⾯竞争的微妙处境。
The Information 本⽉ 11 ⽇周⼆报道称，英伟达⽬标是将 Nemotron 4 最⼤版本的性能打造⾄与全

球顶尖开源模型⽐肩的⽔准。多名员⼯透露，该模型参数量预计⾄少达到⼀万亿 —— 约为其现
有最⼤模型 Nemotron 3 Ultra 的两倍。英伟达⽣成式 AI 副总裁 Kari Briski 在⼀封邮件中表
⽰， " 英伟达投资 Nemotron ，是因为我们相信每家企业、每个国家都需要可及的前沿开源模
型。 "
英伟达当前的芯⽚需求⾼度集中于 OpenAI 、微软、 SpaceX 等少数前沿实验室和云服务商，⽽上
述机构正⽇益布局⾃研 AI 芯⽚。 Nemotron 4 的推进旨在将需求基础拓展⾄更⼴泛的企业⽤⼾群
体，从⽽降低英伟达对头部客⼾的依赖。 AI 模型评测机构 Arena 的⾸席执⾏官 Anastasios
Angelopoulos 表⽰， " ⽆论哪家公司做出优秀的开源模型，英伟达都是赢家。 "
消息传出后，英伟达股价在美股盘前涨近 2% ，但开盘后⾼开低⾛，盘初曾涨超 2% ，午盘转跌。
传出消息当天，英伟达官宣⾯向⻓期运⾏ AI 智能体（ Agent ）的 Nemotron 3.5 Lightning ，并推

出开源模型路由库 NeMo Switchyard ，显⽰该司在加快 Nemotron 模型⽣态布局。戴尔同⽇宣布
扩⼤与英伟达的合作，将这两项技术纳⼊ Dell Deskside Agentic AI 平台，推动智能体在企业本
地及桌⾯端部署。
从万亿参数级前沿模型，到 30 亿活跃参数的⾼效智能体模型，再到模型路由和企业本地基础设
施，英伟达正在形成⼀条从模型、软件到部署平台的完整 AI ⽣态链。此次 Nemotron 4 的研发消
息，也意味着英伟达不再满⾜于做全球最⼤的 AI 芯⽚供应商，⽽是在向开源模型领域的顶级玩家
发起正⾯竞争。
千亿参数⽬标，算⼒投⼊三倍跃升
据多名参与 Nemotron 项⽬的员⼯透露，英伟达计划开发的最⼤规模 Nemotron 4 模型参数量⾄
少达到 1 万亿，约为其今年 6 ⽉发布的现有最⼤模型 Nemotron 3 Ultra 的两倍。该模型参数规模
仍将远低于中国头部开源模型，但英伟达同时强调其压缩技术可使较⼩模型实现超越性的性能
表现。
在算⼒投⼊层⾯，英伟达的决⼼体现于其资⾦承诺的⼤幅扩张。据悉，该公司通过向购买其芯

⽚的云服务商回租 AI 服务器获取算⼒。截⾄今年 4 ⽉，英伟达多年期云服务承诺的总价值已增⾄
2031 年初的 280 亿美元，约为⼀年前披露⾦额的三倍。其中针对本财年（截⾄ 2028 年 1 ⽉）的相
关⽀出约为 70 亿美元，虽远低于 OpenAI 和 Anthropic 的投⼊规模，但已超过绝⼤多数美国和中国
领先开源实验室的历史融资总额。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

在研发⼈员规模上， Nemotron 项⽬同样持续扩张。英伟达上⼀个重要模型的研究论⽂署名作者
多达 570 ⼈，多名员⼯表⽰ Nemotron 4 的参与⼈数将更多。 " 现在每个⼈都想参与进来， " ⼀位前
员⼯说。
组建 "Nemotron 联盟 " ，⼴纳开源⽣态伙伴
为加速 Nemotron 4 的研发，英伟达构建了⼀个名为 "Nemotron 联盟 " 的合作⽹络，成员包括
Reflection 、 Cursor 、 Thinking Machines 和 Mistral 等开源开发商，各⽅在推进⾃⾝模型项⽬的
同时，也向 Nemotron 4 贡献训练数据、评估⽀持和模型设计⽅案。
此外，据⼀位直接参与合作的⼈⼠透露，AI 模型训练初创公司 Prime Intellect 已向该项⽬贡献了
30 万个模拟环境，⽤于辅助模型训练。AI 编程⼯具初创公司 Cognition 也在联盟成员之列，据知
情⼈⼠透露，该公司已就向英伟达提供代码训练数据展开讨论，但相关合作尚未正式对外公

布。
联盟成员的参与动机各异。部分公司希望借助英伟达对开源⽣态的推动扩⼤市场份额；另⼀些
则期望以较低成本影响⼀个可供⾃⾝使⽤的⾼质量模型的研发⽅向，从⽽规避⾃⾏承担训练算
⼒成本的压⼒。 Prime Intellect ⾸席执⾏官 Vincent Weisser 将这⼀联盟定位为对抗单⼀ " 神级 " 模
型垄断的集体⾏动，⽽⾮⼀场争夺最佳开源模型名号的零和博弈。
客⼾与竞争对⼿的边界趋于模糊
Nemotron 4 的推进令英伟达陷⼊⼀种微妙的结构性张⼒之中。⼀⽅⾯， OpenAI ⻓期以来是英伟
达芯⽚需求的重要驱动⽅，英伟达已向其投资 300 亿美元；另⼀⽅⾯， Nemotron 4 的定位恰恰
是为企业提供成本更低的替代⽅案，与 OpenAI 等前沿实验室的商业模型形成竞争。与此同时，
联盟中包含的 Reflection AI 和 Thinking Machines 等公司，也是英伟达已注资的开源创业企业。
不过，对英伟达⽽⾔，这⼀竞争逻辑并不必然是零和游戏。 AI 模型评估机构 Arena 的⾸席执⾏官

Anastasios Angelopoulos 表⽰： " ⽆论哪家公司做出优秀的开源模型，英伟达都是赢家。 " 英伟
达的核⼼逻辑在于：开源⽣态越繁荣、参与主体越多元， GPU 的整体需求就越旺盛。
⽬前， Nemotron 系列模型已获得⼀定市场采⽤，帕兰提尔（ Palantir ）于今年 6 ⽉宣布与英伟达
合作，将 Nemotron 模型⽤于美国政府客⼾。但据 Arena 和 Artificial Analysis 等机构的基准测试数
据，英伟达现有最⼤模型 Nemotron 3 Ultra 在美国开源模型中仅排名第⼆，位列 Thinking
Machines 新发布的 Inkling 模型之后，在全球模型整体排名中更位于前 40 名之外。
发布时间表仍不明朗
尽管项⽬规模宏⼤， Nemotron 4 的发布时间仍存在较⼤不确定性。多名员⼯表⽰，英伟达已就
预训练数据和架构做出若⼲决策，但尚未最终确定具体规格和发布⽇期，最终训练阶段亦尚未
启动，预计耗时数⽉。两名员⼯认为模型最快可在今年秋末发布，另有员⼯认为时间可能更

晚。
与此同时，英伟达本周已发布了 Nemotron 系列的⼀个较⼩版本 ——Nemotron 3.5 Lightning ，该
模型主打以尽可能快的速度和效率运⾏ AI 代理任务。英伟达同步发布了免费的模型路由软件，协
助企业更便捷地构建⾃定义模型路由⼯具，将特定 AI 任务⾃动分配⾄最优、最经济的模型处理。
英伟达应⽤深度学习研究副总裁 Bryan Catanzaro 今年 1 ⽉在⼀档播客中表⽰，投资 Nemotron" 对
我们公司的未来⾄关重要 " 。英伟达⾸席执⾏官⻩仁勋也在上⽉的⼀封邮件中公开表态⽀持开
源，称开源模型 " 促进安全、⽹络安全、科学进步和国家安全 " 。

### Page 3

Nemotron 3.5 先⾏： 30 亿活跃参数专攻⻓期运⾏智能体
就在 Nemotron 4 消息曝光的同⼀天，英伟达正式发布 Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 。
与 Nemotron 4 追求 “ 更⼤ ” 不同， Lightning 追求的是更⾼效。
该模型采⽤混合专家（ MoE ）架构，总参数量约 300 亿，但每个 Token 实际激活约 30 亿参数，主
要针对需要⻓期运⾏、反复调⽤模型的 AI 智能体。英伟达称， Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 最⾼可实
现约 4 倍的 Token ⽣成速度提升。
其定位并不是取代最强⼤的前沿模型，⽽是成为多智能体系统中的 “ 执⾏型 ” 模型。
例如，在⼀个复杂的企业 AI 系统中，⼤模型可以负责任务规划和复杂推理，⽽ Nemotron 3.5
Lightning 则可以承担⼤量代码审查、安全监控、数据处理、告警分析等专业化⼯作。
这也是 MoE 架构的价值所在：模型拥有较⼤的总参数规模，但每次推理只激活其中⼀⼩部分，

从⽽降低单次 Token 计算成本。
英伟达希望通过这⼀设计解决 AI 智能体规模化运⾏后的现实问题 ——⼀个智能体可能不是调⽤模
型⼀次，⽽是连续调⽤⼏⼗次甚⾄数百次。
因此，对于智能体⽽⾔，模型 “ ⾜够聪明 ” 固然重要，但 “ ⾜够便宜、⾜够快 ” 同样重要。
不过， 4 倍 Token ⽣成速度并不意味着完整智能体任务可以提速 4 倍。
第三⽅测试显⽰， Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 在纯模型⽣成环节的速度优势⾮常明显，但放⼊完整
的智能体⼯作流后，整体任务速度提升约 30% 。原因在于，模型推理只是 Agent 系统的⼀部分，
⼯具调⽤、 API 响应以及多个 Agent 之间的任务编排都可能形成新的瓶颈。
这也为英伟达同时推出 NeMo Switchyard 埋下伏笔。
从 “ 模型 ” 到 “ 路由 ” ：英伟达开始解决智能体系统瓶颈
NeMo Switchyard 是⼀套开源模型路由库。
它的核⼼思路是：开发者不需要重写原有应⽤，就可以根据不同任务的特点，把请求⾃动分配

给最合适的模型。
例如，复杂推理任务可以交给⼤型模型；代码审查、分类、数据提取等⾼频任务则可以交给
Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 这样的⾼效模型。
这样做的意义在于，企业不必让每⼀个智能体任务都使⽤最昂贵的模型。
对于未来的多智能体系统⽽⾔，真正重要的可能并不是 “ 哪个模型最好 ” ，⽽是哪个任务应该由哪
个模型完成。
英伟达此次把 Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 与 Switchyard 同时发布，实际上体现了其对智能体基础设
施的判断：随着模型数量越来越多，模型之间的调度和协同将成为新的软件层。
⽽这⼀层恰恰可以帮助英伟达扩⼤⾃⾝的⽣态影响⼒。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
即便企业最终同时使⽤其他⼚商的模型，只要这些模型进⼊英伟达的路由、推理和部署体系，
英伟达依然可以从整个 AI ⼯作流中获得价值。
戴尔扩⼤合作：把 Nemotron 和 Switchyard 带到企业桌⾯
英伟达的模型战略正在进⼀步向企业端落地。
8 ⽉ 11 ⽇，戴尔宣布扩⼤与英伟达的合作，将 Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 和 NeMo Switchyard 纳⼊
Dell Deskside Agentic AI 平台。戴尔称，这⼀平台⾯向企业级智能体应⽤，强调本地运⾏、低
延迟、成本控制和数据安全。
这意味着，英伟达当天的动作并⾮孤⽴的模型发布。
⼀端是 Nemotron 4 这样瞄准万亿参数级别的前沿开源模型；另⼀端则是 Nemotron 3.5 Lightning

这样⾯向本地和企业 Agent ⼯作负载的⼩型⾼效模型。
戴尔则进⼀步把后者与本地硬件和企业 IT 环境结合起来。
对于企业客⼾⽽⾔，本地运⾏ Agent 的吸引⼒在于，敏感数据⽆需频繁离开企业内部，同时可以
降低对云端 API 的依赖，并获得更加可控的延迟和推理成本。
戴尔此前就将 Deskside Agentic AI 定位为连接企业桌⾯端与数据中⼼的⼊⼝。其⽅案基于 Dell AI
Factory with NVIDIA ，并⽀持从本地 Agent 开发逐步扩展到数据中⼼规模部署。
因此，戴尔此次把 Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 和 Switchyard 纳⼊平台，实际上强化了英伟达的另⼀
条路线：
让 Agent 不仅在云端运⾏，也能直接进⼊企业员⼯的⼯作环境。
如果未来越来越多企业采⽤ “ 本地⼩模型 + 云端⼤模型 ” 的混合模式，那么英伟达可以同时从
GPU 、模型、路由软件以及企业基础设施多个环节获得收益。

The Week Ahead July 31, 2026

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Reserve Bank of India policy committee 
(5 Aug)

The Reserve Bank of India monetary 
policy committee (MPC) is expected to 
keep the benchmark rate unchanged at 
5.25% on 5  Aug. We anticipate a 
cautious policy statement, that 
underscores the need for continued 
vigilance on the inflation outlook, while 
placing greater emphasis on core 
inflation as a more reliable measure of 
underlying price pressures than headline 
inflation, w hich has been influenced by 
both domestic and global supply -side 
factors. Against this backdrop, the 
committee is likely to push back a gainst 
market expectations of a more 
aggressive tightening path reflected in 
implied rates.

That said, the MPC is increasingly 
confronted by signs of a build -up in 
inflationary risks. Brent crude prices 
have risen amid renewed geopolitical 
tensions. Below-normal rainfall presents 
upside risks to food inflation, and the 
development of El Niño conditions could 
lead to higher imported agricultural 
commodity prices. Therefore, a more 
hawkish bias in policy guidance may 
become increasingly difficult to avoid as 
the real policy rate cushion narrows, 
inflation risks remain skewed to the 
upside, and the p robability of GDP 
growth exceeding 7% in 1QFY27 
increases.

For the path ahead,  markets will be 
watchful of developments in West Asia 
and signs of any change in the US 
Federal Reserve’s policy outlook in the 
second half of 2026. These 
developments will have important 
implications for the rupee, capital flows , 
and interest rate differentials. In the near 
term, the anticipated recovery in 
portfolio inflows, together with 
continued inflows via the swap windows, 
should provide a constructive backdrop 
for onshore markets.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

China

Exports growth is expected to moderate 
from 27.0% yoy in June to 17.0% in July, 
as renewed tensions in the Middle East 
weighed on external demand. High -
frequency indicators suggest export 
momentum has eased, with total 
deadweight tonnage handled at China's  
20 major ports slowing from 12.9% yoy in 
June to 12.1% in July. Meanwhile, 
international cargo flights declined 26.7% 
to an average of 9,468 per week in July, 
pointing to softer trade activity.

Hong Kong

Retail sales growth is expected to 
accelerate from 7.9% yoy in May to 8.5% 
in June, supported by resilient tourism 
activity and improving domestic 
consumption. Mainland visitor arrivals 
remained robust, growing 10.4% yoy in 
June. Meanwhile, the growth in residents' 
outbound departures eased from 8.1% 
yoy in May to 5.2% in June, suggesting 
fewer residents spending abroad and a

### Page 3

The Week Ahead July 31, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

partial shift in consumption back to the 
domestic market.

South Korea

July inflation data are due for release. 
Headline CPI is expected to remain 
elevated at 3.3% y oy, up slightly from 
3.2% in June and well above the Bank of 
Korea's 2% inflation target. The rebound 
in global oil prices, driven by renewed 
tensions in the Middle East, is likely to 
keep imported inflationary pressures 
elevated. Demand-side inflation remains 
relatively contained, reflecting the 
impact of the BOK's pre -emptive 
monetary tightening in anchoring 
inflation expectations.  We expect CPI 
inflation to rema in in the 3.0 –3.5% y oy 
range throughout 2H26, which should 
prompt the BOK to deliver an additional 
50bps of rate hikes, taking the policy 
rate to 3.25% by year-end.

Taiwan

July trade and inflation data are due for 
release. Export growth is expected to 
remain strong at around 40% y oy, 
resulting in a robust trade surplus of 
USD15bn. Excluding price effects, 
however, export growth is likely to have 
moderated to around 20% y oy. We 
maintain our view that the AI -driven 
export supercycle has peaked and is 
transitioning toward a more normali sed 
growth trajectory.

CPI inflation is expected to remain 
elevated at 2.5% y oy. The rebound in oil 
prices amid renewed Middle East 
tensions is likely to keep imported raw 
material costs elevated, while the severe 
typhoon in July is also expected to have 
pushed up fresh food prices. We 
continue to expect CPI inflation to 
remain in the 2.0 –2.5% y oy range 
through 2H26, prompting the central 
bank to deliver a 12.5bp s rate hike in 
4Q26.

Indonesia and Philippines

Inflation and 2Q26 growth numbers are 
due in early -August. For Indonesia, we 
expect 2Q26 growth to moderate to 5.3% 
yoy from 1Q’s 5.6%. Momentum at the 
beginning of the year was jumpstarted 
by religious festive spending and strong 
increase in public spending. Entering the 
second quarter, fiscal spending 
remained supportive, complemented by 
stimulus measures and the limited pass-
through of elevated global energy prices 
to household consumption, as domestic 
fuel prices remained unchanged. Private 
sector busin ess activity was likely 
impacted by Middle East hostilities, 
shifts in domestic policy architecture , 
and subdued trade sector performance.

Growth in Philippines is expected to 
show signs of bottoming out but remain 
at modest levels, with 2Q output set to 
register 3% yoy rise. Inflation outcomes 
will benefit from a temporary pullback in 
global energy prices, but food costs are 
set to turn dearer on El Nino risks in 
Philippines as well as Indonesia. BSP is 
expected to tighten policy rates, while BI 
will opt to keep rates on hold after back-
to-back increases.

### Page 4

The Week Ahead July 31, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         4

Thailand

We expect Thailand’s headline inflation 
to accelerate to 3.0% yoy in July, the 
upper end of the central bank’s 1 -3% 
target range, from 2.4% yoy in June. The 
pickup reflected firmer food price 
increases amid low base effects, and 
higher core inflation due t o the pass -
through of energy costs. Energy -driven 
inflation likely remained elevated as 
global and domestic fuel prices 
rebounded following the escalation of 
tensions in the Middle East. Nonetheless, 
the Bank of Thailand is unlikely to rush 
into monetary t ightening, as it looks 
through this supply -driven increase in 
inflation, balancing with concerns about 
uneven economic growth.

Vietnam

We expect Vietnam’s goods exports to 
expand by 25.5% yoy in July, extending 
the strong growth of 28.1% yoy in June, 
supported by robust electronics 
shipments benefitting from spillovers 
from artificial intelligence -related 
demand, despite weakness in texti les, 
garments, and footwear exports. Retail 
sales likely picked up amid resilient 
domestic consumption and solid tourism 
arrivals, despite elevated inflation. We 
anticipate headline inflation to rise to 5.0% 
yoy in July from 4.7% yoy in June, driven 
by fir mer transport price increases as 
global and domestic fuel prices 
rebounded following the escalation of 
tensions in the Middle East, alongside 
persistently elevated food inflation.

《亞馬遜業績亮眼 ，零售、廣告、AWS業務齊成
長，AI投資成功轉獲利》
亞馬遜2Q26業績成長且優於市場預期 。受惠於AI
算力需求大爆發，雲端服務AWS業務部門營收飆升
37%創下18季以來的最快增速，以及零售與廣告業
務同步成長 ，再疊加對 AI新創公司 Anthropic股權
投資的帳面估值增值，帶動整體業績大幅擊敗預期。
即便扣除 Anthropic股權投資帶來的紙上富貴 ，營
業利益仍是年增 43% ，AWS營 業 利 潤 率 提 升 至

39.4%，皆證明了亞馬遜的鉅額投資正在取得成效 。
而不斷擴增的積壓訂單，呼應了管理層所強調「供
不應求」的市場現況。因記憶體價格上漲，公司亦
將全年資本支出上調至2200億美元。

亮眼，皇家加勒比郵輪(RCL)2Q26業績優於公司預

《高生活成本擠壓下，餐飲業持續面臨消費挑戰，
此時，企業經營能力成為未來獲利決勝點》
星巴克2Q26業績亮眼並優於預期 ，全球同店銷售
額成長得益於交易量及平均客單價增長 。營業利潤
率成長得益於銷售槓桿效應和較低的通貨膨脹率以

及關稅退款 。星巴克上調 2026財年展望 ，預期同
店銷售額成長6.5%或更高，非GAAP合併營業利益
率大於11%，非GAAP每股盈餘預計在 2.55美元至
2.65美元之間。重返星巴克的轉型計劃持續展現成
果，使星巴克連續四季實現同店銷售額成長 ，連續
兩季實現利潤率擴張。管理層表示業績反映了公司
在營收和利潤方面表現的日益增強的可持續性 ，對
公司的未來發展方向充滿信心。
2Q26麥當勞業績好壞參半 ，營收成長但遜於預期 ，
調整後每股盈餘成長但優於預期 。美國同店銷售成
長得益於客單價的正面貢獻 ，但部分被客流量的下
降所抵銷，國際直營同店銷售成長得益於德國 、澳
洲和英國表現，部份被法國市場所抵銷，國際加盟
同店銷售成長得益於日本，但被中國市場的下滑部
份抵銷。儘管每股盈餘的超預期表現 ，為不被看好
的業績帶來寬慰，但營收不如預期與同店銷售增速
放緩仍點出消費環境依然充滿挑戰 ，該公司針對低
收入消費者的價值行銷策略收效甚微 ，除了商品與
燃料帶來的高生活成本使這些消費者減少外食支出 ，

美國地區門市在策略執行力上不彰也成為業績表現
不佳的檢討重點。
• 高生活成本擠壓下 ，餐飲業
持續面臨消費挑戰 ，星巴克
的「回歸星巴克 」策略有效
提升營運效率 ，麥當勞則因
對策略的執行力不彰 ，導致
同店銷售業績成長放緩。

中国
◼ 製造業PMIは拡大ペースが鈍化
６月のRatingDog製造業PMIは51.7と、
前月（51.8）から拡大ペースがわずか
に鈍化。
◼ インフレ率は幾分鈍化
６月の総合CPIは前年比+1.0%と、前月
（同+1.2%）から横ばいで推移。中東
情勢の緊張緩和に伴う原油価格の低下か
ら、ガソリンなど輸送費の伸びが鈍化し、
総合CPIを押し下げた。
◼ 消費は低位で推移
６月の小売売上高は前年比＋1.0%と、
５月（同▲0.6％）からは増加に転じた。
６月の不動産販売面積は同▲12.5％と、
依然として減少が続いており、不動産市
況の低迷が長期化している。
◼ 輸出は増加幅が拡大
６月の輸出は前年比+27.0%と、前月
（同+19.3%）から増加幅が拡大。世界
的なAI投資の拡大を背景に、半導体など
のハイテク製品や機械電気製品の出荷が
増加した。仕向け地別では、前年の裏要
因により米国向けが同+13.9％と増加幅
が縮小したものの、その他の国・地域で
は増加幅が拡大した。

幅が拡大。昨年９月からのGST（物品
サービス税）引き下げや、各社メー
カーが新型車を投入したことから、乗
用車の販売台数が増加。

前月（同＋4.9％）から増加幅が拡大。
製造業や再生可能エネルギーの生産増
加が牽引した。

ン情勢悪化による燃料価格の上昇により、

７月16日、韓国銀行は利上げを決定（＋
25bps、2.50％→2.75％）。利上げは市
場予想通り。2023年１月以来、約３年半
ぶりの利上げとなった。声明文では、
「輸出と投資に牽引された経済成長の強
化に伴い、インフレ率は相当期間目標水
準を上回ると予想される」とし、経済成
長、インフレ率、金融安定性の３つの全
ての領域で利上げの必要性が裏付けられ
るとした。

的なAIデータセンター投資の急増により、

半導体輸出が同+199.5%増加し、輸出を
牽引した。

◼ PMIは引き続き拡大

◼ BSPはインフレ対策で利上げ
６月の総合CPIは前年比＋6.4％と、前月
（同＋6.8％）から幾分鈍化。中東情勢
の緊張緩和に伴い、輸送費のプラス寄与

とも、総合CPIのみならず、コアCPIも同

→4.75％）。利上げは市場予想通り。声
明文では、「本日の政策措置は、インフ
レ期待を安定させ、二次的な影響のリス
クを軽減するのに役立つ」とした。
◼ 新車販売台数は減少幅が縮小
５月の新車販売台数は前年比▲15.7％と、
前月（同▲18.9％）から減少幅が縮小。
中東情勢の悪化に伴う燃料価格の激しい
乱高下が一服したことで、冷え込んでい
た消費マインドと自動車業界の先行き見
通しが改善した。
◼ 輸出は引き続き増加

10年・2年債利回りスプレッド（左軸）
40年・10年債利回りスプレッド（右軸）
（年/月）
（％PT） （％PT）
1. 10年金利の動向と見通し
日本の長期金利（10年債利回り）は、高水準で推移している 。
中東情勢や日銀政策姿勢 、高市政権の財政政策により変
動が大きい展開となったものの、 2.8％を超えるような大幅上
昇には至っていない 。前回レポート で述べたように 、債券市
場のターミナルレート想定値が2 ％を超え、ここからのさらな
る上昇は物価目標を明確に超える政策金利水準の引き上
げとなるが、現段階では想定しづらいことが要因だ 。中東情
勢がエスカレーションしなかったこと 、高市政権の補正予算

規模が想定ほど拡張的ではなかったことも一因とみられる。
今月の注目点は、 6月日銀会合を受けた債券市場の動きで
あったが、総じてみて会合は無風通過となり 、特に状況に大
きな変化はみられない。債券市場のビハインドザカーブ懸念
の代理指標である長短金利スプレッドをみると （図表1）、会
合後は小幅に拡大した。内田副総裁会見では今後の利上げ
ペースに関する追加的な示唆には乏しかったこともあり 、債
券市場の今後の利上げ織り込みに大きな変化はなく 、市場
でのビハインドザカーブ懸念は後退しなかった 。そのため、
今後何らかのきっかけで 、同懸念が要因となって再び長短
スプレッドが拡大するリスクは残ったままだ。
長短金利スプレッドをやや長い目でみると、スプレッドの拡大
傾向が強まった時期は昨年 10月頃からであり 、高市自民党
総裁誕生による政府の経済政策の大幅転換が債券市場で
意識されているようだ。責任ある積極財政の方針を受け 、イ
ンフレ期待や国債需給等が材料視されているとみられる。
昨年10月以降の長期金利を①10年物BEI＋実質金利、②10

年物ASW（10年債利回り－ 10年物OIS、名目タームプレミア
ムに近似)の2軸から評価すると 、まず①については 、BEIに
よる押し上げが 50bp、実質金利が50bp であり、インフレ期待
の寄与がおよそ半分 だ。実質金利には実質タームプレミア
ムも含まれるが、正確な判別はできない。そこで、②をみると、
ASWは概ね横ばい圏で推移している 。よって、実質金利の
上昇を財政懸念によるタームプレミアムの押し上げだけで説
明するのは乱暴だろう 。高市政権の積極財政による成長期
待の高まりや日銀のストック効果縮小など、複数の要因が重
なっていると考えるのが自然だ。
BEIから算出されるインフレ期待は流動性の低さなど様々な
要因から実勢より過小評価されているとの指摘（ 例えばThe
Economistなど）が多い。結論として 、長期金利上昇の主因
はやはりインフレ期待の上昇と言えるのではないか。
2026年6月円金利見通し：ビハインドザカーブ懸念と40年・30年ゾーンの逆イールドについて
 金利の現状：長期金利は2.7％前後で推移。既に債券市場のターミナル想定値は相応に高く、高市政権の

補正予算が懸念ほど拡張的ではなかったことが要因。一方、超長期金利は発行減などから低下傾向。
 今後の見通し：長期金利はビハインドザカーブ懸念から引き続き 2.7％前後の下値の堅い展開が続くと予想。
財政政策動向次第では2.8％越えも。超長期金利は引き続き緩やかな低下傾向での推移を想定。
 40年・30年ゾーンの逆イールド：過去3年40年・30年債利回りスプレッドの急激な縮小が5月に発生しており、
毎年6月のPD懇に向けた発行減額観測が、特に40年債利回りを押し下げているとみられる。

26/01 26/02 26/03 26/04 26/05 26/06 （年/月）
5年先5年物インフレスワップ（左軸）
債券市場想定の中立金利と
ターミナルレートのスプレッド（右軸）
（％） （％PT）
10年債・2年債利回りスプレッド（右軸）
今後のインフレ期待をみるうえで 、インフレスワップをやや仔
細にみると、実は5年先5年物が、5月中旬に一段切り上がっ

ている。債券市場では足下よりも中期的なインフレ期待が高
まっているということだ。 そのため、6月中旬以降に中東情勢
改善期待が高まり 、原油高が一服している中でも 、10年物
BEIならびに長期金利が大きく低下に転じる兆しは乏しい。
中長期のインフレ期待が一段上昇した時期に 、債券市場の
想定する中立金利とターミナルレートの乖離が一段と大きく
なった（図表2）。つまり日銀ビハインドザカーブ懸念 （ターミナ
ルレート想定値の上昇以上に中立金利想定値が上昇）によっ
て中長期インフレ期待が押し上げられたことがわかる。
同時期に一段とビハインドザカーブ懸念が高まった理由を一
つに特定することは困難だが、 為替介入後のドル円レートが
再び円安となったことも関係しているかもしれない 。いずれに
せよ、今後
インフレ期待が抑制されて長期金利上昇に歯止め
がかかるかどうかは 、日銀の物価目標達成に向けた市場の
信認が得られるかどうか にかかっていると言える 。弊社井上
エグゼクティブエコノミストのレポート で指摘されているように 、

6月会合の結果では今後の利上げペースが加速するかどう
かは判然としなかった 。そのため、債券市場では当面 、日銀
のビハインドザカーブ懸念が燻り 、これが長期金利を押し上
げ、2.7％前後の下値の堅い展開が続くと予想される。
ただし、7月の注目はむしろ財政政策の動向だ。7月には骨太
の方針が公表される見込みで 、すでに戦略17分野への官民
合計370兆円（2040年度まで）の投資戦略が策定されると報じ
られる。財源の議論の内容次第では 、財政リスクが意識され
て長期金利は2.8％越えを試す展開となろう 。加えて、FRBの
利上げ観測の高まりにより円安地合いが強まり 、日銀の早期
利上げ観測も高まるかもしれない 。既に一部の識者からは9
～10月会合の利上げ可能性が指摘される 。当社のハウス
ビューでは、日銀6月会合の内容などを踏まえ 、日銀は利上
げ後の金融環境やインフレ動向の見極めに時間をかけると
予想し
、次回利上げ時期の予想は12月のまま不変だ。
2. 40年金利の動向と見通し
40年金利は、10年金利の上昇が押し上げ要因となるものの、

前回レポート時点 （5月14日）から明確に低下し 、超長期・長
期金利スプレッドも縮小傾向 にある（前頁図表1）。以前のレ
ポートで指摘した ①外国人などの一部投資家による買い需
要、②日銀の国債買入れ比率上昇による需給悪化懸念後退、
③会計ルール緩和期待、④株高によるリバランス需要などの
要因が挙げられる。高市政権の補正予算規模が懸念ほど拡
張的ではなかった点や、 日銀の6 月会合で国債買入れ減額
停止が決まった点（ 弊社井上エグゼクティブエコノミストのレ
ポートをご参照）も、追い風となったと推察される。
先行きも、財務省の発行減など政府・ 日銀による超長期市場
安定化策への期待から、超長期金利は緩やかな低下傾向で
推移すると予想 している。ただし、買い手不足による需給軟
調や高市政権の積極財政に対する懸念、 米利上げなどによ
る海外の超長期金利上昇への警戒感などを受け 、3.8％前後
の高水準圏での推移を予想している。
注目されるのは40年・30年債利回りの逆イールドだ（図表3）。
超長期ゾーンの中でも 、40年債の相対的なアウトパフォーム

が目立つ展開となっている。
逆イールドの要因は季節性ではないか。マイナス金利解除以
降の40年・30年金利スプレッドをみると、毎年5月になるとスプ
レッドが大幅に縮小し、それが7月頭ぐらいまで続く。3年連続
同じ事象が発生しているということは、 季節性があるとみてよ
いだろう。背景には、毎年6月に行われるPD懇に向けて、5月
頃から超長期国債の発行減額観測が高まっていたことがあ
る。このうち、40年債は他の年限と比べて発行減額が進んで
いる（今年の40年債発行予定額は 30年債の4分の1にとどま
る）こともあり 、需給ひっ迫 （国債発行減） 観測の影響を相対
的に受けやすいとみられる 。需要面から見れば、報道では昨
年の円金利高騰を受けた投資抑制が一巡し 、一部の生保の
超長期債に投資する動きが出始めているようだ 。その影響が
加わったことで 、今年6月に逆イールドが発生したとみられる 。
特に、超長期ゾーンについては日銀が買入れオペ額を減額
させず維持していたこともあり 、今年の発行減額観測はより

40年債の利回りを押し下げたと推察される 。先行きも、発行
減額期待が続けば逆イールドは当面続きそうだが、 毎年7月
になるとスプレッドは再び拡大している 。おそらく骨太の方針
などを受けた財政拡張観測が高まりやすい季節性があるの
だろう。前述したように 、今年の骨太方針と国債発行計画へ
の影響は大変な注目を集めており、財政リスクが高まれば逆
イールドが解消する可能性もある 。例年のパターン通りであ
れば、スプレッドはゼロ近傍で推移するのではないか。
なお、この40年・30年の逆イールドの発生は特別な現象と捉
えるべきではない 。約10年前にも逆イールドが発生したこと
がある点や、足元の海外金利（40年債利回りを発行している
ヨーロッパ諸国 ）をみても逆イールドは発生している 。グロー
バルでも40年債が選好されて利回りが低下していることが 、
日本の40年債利回り低下の一因だろう。背景には株高による
リバランス需要や長寿化対応があるのかもしれない。ただし、
米国は40 年債利回りを発行していないため 、上記要因は逆

イールドの追加的な材料にすぎないだろう。
2
図表2：インフレ期待と債券市場想定・中立金利とターミナ
ルレート想定値のスプレッド、長短金利スプレッド

## Document 431: 中国の貿易統計（26年7月）～輸出入ともに減速、半導体関連は高い伸びを維持

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国の貿易統計（26年7月）～輸出入ともに減速、半導体関連は高い伸びを維持.pdf
Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-08-07
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

１．概況 
・中国海関総署が 2026 年 8 月 7 日に 26 年 7 月の貿易統計の速報値を発表した。財輸出 （ドル建て、
図表 1）は前年同月比＋23.9％となった（前月は同＋27.0％）。財輸入は同＋27.5％となった（前月
は同＋36.0％）。貿易収支は＋1,125 億ドルと、前年同月（＋982 億ドル）から増加した。当社試算
の季節調整値でみた前月比は、輸出が前月比▲3.5％、輸入が同▲1.7％であった。 
・財輸出（人民元建て、図表 2）は前年同月比＋17.8％となった（前月は同＋20.8％）。財輸入は同＋
21.2％となった（前月は同＋29.4％） 。貿易収支は＋7,671 億元と、前年同月（＋7,051 億元）から
増加した。

２．詳細 
（１）輸出 
・国・地域別（ドル建て、前年同月比）では、米国向けが前年増を続けており、前月からはやや加速
した。ASEAN 向け、日本向けは加速した一方、EU 向けは減速した（図表 3）。 
・品目別（同上）では、プラスチック製品や紡績糸等、鋼材、家電、自動車、自動車部品など、主要
品目の多くが減速した。衣類等やコンピュータ・同部品は加速した。集積回路も減速したものの、
価格高騰の影響で 3 桁を超える伸びが続いている（図表 4）。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-08-07

市場展望投資策略
 息收角度出發，CSP投等債無須擔憂：聯準會政策不確
定性，疊加未來長債增發風險，長端殖利率居高不下，
債券仍以息收角度出發。儘管CSP上調資本支出，然而
預期2028年自由現金流有望好轉，相關持債無須擔憂。
 短線科技股靈活操作，留意資金控管：最壞情境看似已
過，然而台股融資餘額仍偏高，韓股隱含波動度仍高，
短線須留意再次回檔可能。中長線則持續樂觀看待科技
股表現。(詳見Page 15)
 買進美國金融股 ：殖利率曲線陡峭持續有利銀行股 NII
擴張，同時資本市場的大波動也有利投行業務的成長動
能。此外，Visa、Mastercard財報持續釋出樂觀展望，
疊加估值相對大盤仍偏低 ，漲勢有望延續 。(詳見Page
17)
 匯市展望：美、日聯合進場干預日圓 ，引發日圓強勢升
值，短期做空日圓的投資交易情緒暫時消退 ，美元/日圓
有向下回測1月152低點的可能。美元指數受到日圓轉強
影響 ，疊加油價下滑 ，動能暫歇 ，本週美元指數區間

98.5~100.3。(詳見Page 9、10)
 債市展望：美伊談判傳出利多消息 ，引導油價回檔 ，十
年殖利率高位下滑 。然而，長端殖利率在貨幣政策不確
定性和財政疑慮多重因素干擾下 ，預期下行幅度有限 。
本週十年殖利率區間4.55%~4.75%。(詳見Page 12)
 股市展望 :近期股市反彈，漲勢集中在CSP、軟體類股，
顯示市場資金並非全面重新流回半導體 ，而是持續尋找
低基期標的。本週S&P500區間7,600~7,800。
本頁面公司、ETF名稱僅為列示，非代表單一個股推薦

### Page 5

美國財政部上調舉債規模
5
財政部調高借款規模 圖表解讀
➢ 8月美國財政部揭露季度發債計畫 ，相較於前次 5/4預
估，上調Q3 7~9月淨融資規模至7,390億美元(較前次
預估上調 680億美元)，主因為預期現金流的下降 。財
政部維持各天期拍賣規模不變 ，儘管貝森特先前曾提
及增發長債的計畫 ，但在近期長端殖利率走強的壓力
下，仍將利用國庫券融資因應 ，財政部聲明中維持未
來數個季度付息債拍賣規模不變。
➢ 然而隨著政府財政赤字越趨龐大 ，摩根大通預估 2027
財年(2026/10/1~2027/9/30)開始 ，財政部便會擴大
季度融資拍賣規模，且未來數年將越趨龐大。
➢ 美 國 運 用 國 庫 券 融 資 比 重 已 經 超 過 20% ， 超過
TBAC(借款顧問委員會 )建議的 15~20%，因此增發付
息債勢在必行 ，也是未來長端殖利率在供給增加下 ，
較難下行的主因之一。
資料來源：美國財政部, 2026/8/3、JPM, 2026/7/28

重點提醒

的主因。

### Page 9

日圓短期偏強，但根本問題依舊在利差
9
2011/3 G7聯合干預，日圓後續仍升值 圖表解讀
➢ 7月底美 、日政府聯合干預日圓貶值 ，引發美元 /日圓
迅速從近年高位向下升值 。相較於2022、2024年兩次
日本政府單獨的行動 ，此次美國政府的加入 ，讓市場
擔憂日圓是否會如同 2024/7~2024/9啟動一波大幅升
值的波段走勢。
➢ 然而，2024/7日圓升值主因為當時 BoJ意外升息 ，引
發後續貨幣政策緊縮預期 ，與本次情境並不相同 。這
次干預更像是 2011/3，當時由於日本大地震 ，保險公
司、企業將資金大量匯回國內用於重建 ，推升日圓升
值，也導致G7試圖聯合干預升值趨勢 。然而，該次干
預結果最終只引導日圓貶值約 1個月的時間，後續日圓
重新回到原先的升值趨勢。
➢ 換句話說，本次干預行動將暫時引導日圓偏強 (短期有
望向下回測150~152價位)，但要根本性扭轉弱勢日圓 ，
依舊得依靠BoJ快速升息，拉近美日利差。
資料來源：Bloomberg, 2026/8/4

### Page 10

日圓干預牽動短期美元動能疲弱
10
油價回落同步引導美元動能暫歇 圖表解讀
➢ 川普取消 「二戰以來最大軍事打擊 」，同時宣稱與伊
朗談判正在進行中(伊朗否認)，降低地緣政治衝突，油
價隨之回落 ，也引導美元指數同步走跌 。近年美元動
能與油價高度綁定 ，主要反應油價驅動美國通膨走勢 。
➢ 自3月以來，伊朗戰略明確，在賭注美國不敢派遣地面
部隊進攻下 ，採取拖延戰術的方式 ，持續消耗美國 ，
最終希望美國妥協荷姆茲海峽的掌控權 。因此，美伊
局勢恐陷入長時間僵局 ，油價、美元也將隨消息進展
波動。
➢ 短期美元指數焦點還是回歸到日圓干預的影響 ，走勢
偏弱。儘管美債殖利率向上走勢較歐債 、日債更強 ，
但主要由聯準會政策不確定因素帶動 ，因此支撐效果
有限，本週美元指數區間98.5~100.3。

### Page 11

油價利好殖利率下行，然留意債務壓力重啟
11
債市表現說明
➢ 美國公債/投等債：上週油價下滑 ，同步引導殖利率由高
檔下行 ，激勵公債 、投等債走勢 。然而，全球財政壓力
的潛在風險 ，依舊可能引發殖利率走升的連鎖效應 。近
期惠譽將法國信評由 AA-調降為A+，反應上半年赤字高
出預期14.4%，和2026年赤字率可能高達8%的風險(歐盟
上限為3%)。
➢ 美國非投資等級債： 受到股市反彈激勵 ，上週非投等債
指數同步走強 。信用利差方面 ，BB利差依舊維持低檔 ，
然而CCC及以下評級則自 5月以來持續走揚 ，兩者走勢分
化嚴重，建議避開CCC以下持股比重相對較高的標的。
➢ 新興市場當地債： 中國政治局會議釋放刺激訊號 ，十年
殖利率向下走低 ，疊加美元指數動能轉弱 ，利好新當債
表現。

### Page 12

長端利率仍將受不確定性影響維持高檔
12
圖表解讀
➢ 美伊戰爭爆發以來 ， 美國一共揭露 4次通膨數據 (3月
~6月)，其中權重占比 60%的核心服務從 2月年增率貢
獻1.77%上升至6月的1.91%，而權重占比6%的能源貢
獻卻從0.03%上升至1.01%。因此，期間美國 CPI的上
漲幾乎可以說是完全由能源價格上漲導致 ，若扣除能
源衝擊，美國並未有通膨上升風險。
➢ Warsh之所以想修改通膨觀察指標 ，便是希望不要因
為短期供應的衝擊影響對於通膨的判斷 ，也是其上任
至今，儘管6、7月FOMC會議上釋出致力於 2%通膨目
標，但並未升息的關鍵。
➢ Warsh取消前瞻指引的做法 ，讓市場對於政策走向陷
入極大不確定 ，導致殖利率曲線陡峭化 (長端利率要求
更多風險溢酬 )。當前美國通膨變化完全綁定美伊之間
的關係走向，美伊之間恐陷入長期的膠著 ，預期9月會
議上，Warsh仍可能維持相同的模糊態度 ，利率曲線
陡峭化的態勢不易改變。
資料來源： Bloomberg, 2026/7/31

### Page 13

金融債為投資首選，同時檢視科技債持有標的
13
圖表解讀
➢ 2022年以來，當殖利率上升或維持高檔盤整階段 ，以
金融、REITs、公用事業產業的投等債總報酬表現較佳 ，
含息報酬率甚至擁有正報酬的空間 。因此，在當前定
調長端殖利率仍將維持高檔一段期間的環境下 ，建議
仍以這幾項產業債為主，同時存續期以中天期為主。
➢ 而在大型雲端服務商相關的債券方面 ，儘管2Q26資本
支出的上調 ，一度引發財務體質惡化 、違約率上升的
擔憂，然而例如:Google、亞馬遜自由現金流量最差的
情境是發生在2027年，2028年隨著雲端業務的營收 、
利潤成長至一定規模 ，已經能抵銷龐大資本支出流出
的資金壓力。
➢ 若是手中持有 5~7年內到期的相關債券不用太過擔心 ，
但若持有長天期標的 ，或者是體質較差的甲骨文債 ，
就需要考慮未來利差 、CDS受消息影響 ，繼續走高的
可能。
資料來源： Bloomberg, 2026/8/3

### Page 14

板塊輪動迅速，低基期族群受資金青睞
14
股市表現說明
➢ 費城半導體指數： 在7月明顯修正後 ，8月初半導體相關
族 群 迎 來 強 勢 反 彈， 然而 AMD 財 測 未 達 高 標， 疊加

源於阿里巴巴 、京東等權值股先前跌幅較重 ，估值修復
迎來資金抄底 。隨著全球股市回穩 ，港股評價面也未再
像先前極具吸引力 ，漲勢驅動轉向中國內需好轉的預期 。
➢ 原材料指數： 礦業次產業在銅價 、金價漲勢的推動下 ，
成為激勵整體原材料指數的關鍵 。LME庫存處在低位 ，
以及期貨市場逆價差證明銅礦供需吃緊可能延續 ，有望
在短期繼續激勵相關類股漲勢。

動率高達5.09%。

可能性 ，資金在金融 、地產、健護等價值股輪動的時
間有望延續 。因此，短線科技股操作須注意靈活性和
資金控管，留意過度重壓風險。

### Page 16

亞馬遜支出回收信心上升，助漲半導體動能
16
雲端業務動能持續加快 圖表解讀
➢ 亞馬遜揭露2Q26財報，其中雲端業務AWS營收增速雖
由1Q26的28%上升至37%，然而近12個月(TTM)自由
現金流量 (FCF)卻由正轉負至 -76億美元 ，同時再度上
調資本支出至2200億美元，對比Google的自由現金流，
亞馬遜的財務體質明顯更加惡化 ，但市場卻給予正面
解讀，與Google財報揭露後表現明顯不同。
➢ 關鍵的差異在於 ，亞馬遜讓市場相信現在的大額投資
是有辦法回收 。CEO明確指出伺服器和網路設備投資
不到3年便能回收，實現收益平衡後的 2~3年可產生可
觀的現金流，重燃市場信心。
➢ 7月半導體的修正很大一部分的主因便是 CSP資本支出
再度上調的疑慮 ，一旦市場重新相信 CSP能快速讓投
資回報率 (ROIC)增加，依照半導體股價跌深 、估值便
宜、訂單趨勢明確角度 ，下半年收復6月跌幅失土的速
度有望加快。
資料來源：Bloomberg, 2026/7/31

### Page 17

金融股前景樂觀，仍有落後補漲空間
17
大型銀行營收仍高於成本增速 圖表解讀
➢ 2Q26大型銀行 (BAC、C、JPM、MS、PNC、USB、
WFC)財報釋出樂觀預期 ，多數銀行強調 Q2強勁的貸
款成長(由Q1的年增6.5%上升至Q2:7.5%)有望持續到
年底，因此市場開始上調2026~2028年大型銀行NII增
速展望(殖利率曲線陡峭有利NII加速成長)。
➢ 儘管大型銀行紛紛上調利息收入預估 ，成本的攀升卻
一度成為財報後壓抑銀行股股價的關鍵 。然而，成本
的上升主要伴隨投行業務的成長和先前投資計畫的執
行，只要營收增速高於成本增速 ，大型銀行ROTCE(有
形普通股權益報酬率)仍將維持成長動能。
➢ Mastercard和Visa 2Q26 業 績 皆 超 預 期， 同 時 維 持
2026年營收成長低雙位數的樂觀展望 。目前金融服務
PE 21.5X，低估值的優勢(5年均值22.2X)容易吸引目前
較不偏好半導體的資金流入。

8月機會市場亮點
18

資產別 市場 亮點
股票
市場
美國
美國經濟與企業財報展望仍呈正向，Q2財報優於預期比例超過長期平均。但隨期中選舉時程將近，
市場波動性預期加劇，加上Fed今年升息可能性持續上升，或抑制高估值類股。在類股輪動下，可
適度布局非科技類股，分散風險。
日本 截至7/30，已公布業績指引的公司中11%上調指引，創下2021年以來新高，基本面穩健。惟須留
意美、日政府聯手干預匯市下，若後續日圓持續強勢升值，可能對股市造成短線上的衝擊。
新亞台灣 AI需求仍維持強勁，台股AI供應鏈優勢猶在，但短期隨韓股漲高後進行去槓桿而造成震盪。持續關
注美股大型科技企業財報，長期展望依舊維持正向，短期則注意部位風險，建議先以大型股為主。
房地產 7月科技股下跌期間，地產類股展現良好的抗震效果，預期8月科技股震盪可能延續下，地產股的防
禦特性有望維持。
科技/通訊 科技仍是美股盈利增長核心引擎，AI資本支出、基礎設施建設與半導體科技巨頭貢獻市場主要利潤
增量，惟須留意短期仍有波動風險，短線部位注意靈活操作和風險控管。

金融 AI熱潮促進證券交易部門業績大增，大型銀行同步受益於資本支出和高利率。隨全球資本支出規模
持續擴張，未來資金流動性將成各家積極爭取的關鍵，有利大型銀行股長期獲利再上層樓。
原材料 超級聖嬰現象預期將放大全球原物料供需失衡問題，使原先已受AI浪潮帶動的化學品價格持續攀升。
此外，美伊紛爭再起，加上俄烏戰爭近期衝突擴大，天然氣、油價波動加劇。
債券
市場 投等債 債券報酬全年預期以息收為主，在利差收斂、價格彈性有限與匯率不確定下，利息成相對穩定且可
控的貢獻來源。美元投等債在高殖利率環境下展現長期投資價值，為降低整體投資組合波動的選擇。
匯率
市場 美元 油價反彈使Fed無法完全排除今年升息的可能，加上歐元、日圓相對疲軟，為美元提供短期支撐。
看好減碼 中立

19
資產別 市場 評論
股票
市場
歐洲 歐元區較難通過AI智能驅動的投資繁榮來抵消拖累，歐洲缺乏推動資本支出的大型科技領軍企業。
能源衝擊對歐洲工業的打擊仍在，加劇輸入成本並壓縮歐洲企業利潤空間，亮點仍在國防及基建。
中國 7月底政治局會議明確指出下半年要實施更加積極的財政政策和適度寬鬆的貨幣政策，刺激內需為
中國經濟下半年的主軸，也成為中國股市內需類股補漲的機會。
健護
醫療
股市動盪修正下，醫療健護類股變成市場資金避風港，隨民主黨有望在期中選舉重返眾院(甚至兩院
全拿)，預期醫保費率將回歸合理增長，有利大型醫保及醫藥企業獲利增長。
工業 美國ISM製造業指數重返擴張，受惠AI基礎建設、資料中心和輸電網路需求旺盛，帶動製造業產能
利用率和訂單回升。AI的運算需求的快速成長，這需要對發電進行持續投資，工業股直接受惠。
能源 能源需求受AI資料中心、電力基建及工業帶動。能源企業現金流及ROE具吸引力，能源股策略配置。
公用
事業
公用事業類股因數據中心帶來的龐大電力需求，已從傳統的防禦型「收息股」轉型為兼具穩健配息

與成長潛力的標的。這類公司具備強大護城河，但高利率環境估值壓力大，採防守與收益型配置。
消費 美國消費具韌性，高所得族群支出穩健，消費朝服務、旅遊及體驗經濟移轉，聚焦穩定現金流企業。
債券
市場
公債 公債具分散風險及提升投組防禦力功能，建議採中性存續期間配置，兼顧收益與未來資本利得機會。
非投
等債 在企業違約率尚可接受下，可分散布局非投等債基金，獲取較投等債高的利息收入來源。
新興
市場
新興市場債殖利率相比全球債券及美國債券更具吸引力；許多新興市場央行已早早升息，通膨開始
回落，本地貨幣債實際殖利率在全球中相當突出，但需謹慎選擇，因匯率波動與政治風險不可輕忽。

## Document 580: 海外宏观周报：非农意外转负，加息预期降温

Source PDF: all report pdf/海外宏观周报：非农意外转负，加息预期降温.pdf
Report source: 中邮证券
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告：宏观报告

市场有风险，投资需谨慎                                                        请务必阅读正文之后的免责条款部分 
研究所

分析师:李起 
SAC 登记编号:S1340524110001 
Email:liqi2@cnpsec.com 
分析师:高晓洁 
SAC 登记编号:S1340526020001 
Email:gaoxiaojie@cnpsec.com  
近期研究报告

《海外宏观周报：欧央行按兵不动，静
待美联储议息会议》 - 2026.07.28   
 宏观观点

海外宏观周报：非农意外转负，加息预期降温

⚫ 核心观点 
上周五公布的美国7 月非农就业数据大幅弱于预期， 加息担忧有
所缓解。7 月非农就业人数减少2.3 万人，明显低于市场预期的增加
8 万人，5 月和6 月新增就业人数合计被下修10.3 万人。 失业率虽然
小幅回落， 但劳动参与率也进一步下降，表明失业率下降部分源于劳
动力退出，并不代表就业状况改善。整体来看，美国就业市场明显降
温，但裁员规模仍然有限，继续呈现低招聘、低裁员的紧平衡状态。
数据公布后，美债收益率回落，美股进一步上涨。 
我们认为， 美国经济正处于增长仍有韧性、 就业边际降温的阶段，
劳动力市场不足以支持美联储收紧货币政策。 在就业动能减弱、 工资
增速放缓的背景下， 再次加息可能会给劳动力市场带来额外的下行压
力。尽管通胀仍高于政策目标，但6 月数据已明显降温，当前的通胀
趋势尚不足以推动美联储加息。我们判断美联储年内不会加息，若中
东局势不再升级、通胀压力不再回升，美联储仍有可能降息一次。 
资产配置方面，政策利率见顶和潜在降息预期对美债构成支撑，
但美联储反应函数不够清晰，期限溢价可能继续处于高位。随着加息
预期降温， 美元的利差支撑趋于减弱， 过去几个月的升值趋势可能面
临逆转。 
⚫ 风险提示： 
若中东局势再度升级并推升油价， 通胀预期可能重新抬升， 市场
对美联储加息及高利率维持更久的定价可能再次升温， 带动美债收益
率上行，并对美元构成支撑。

发布时间：2026-08-11

### Page 4

请务必阅读正文之后的免责条款部分      4 
1 海外宏观事件与数据跟踪

上周海外市场主要围绕科技企业盈利、 美国就业数据和中东局势展开。此前
AI 交易去杠杆带来的技术性压力有所缓解，强劲的企业盈利重新为科技板块提
供支撑。 标普500 指数全周上涨3.6%并创历史新高， 纳斯达克100 指数上涨5.1%，
罗素2000 指数上涨3.5%。从市场结构看，资金由前期较为拥挤的芯片和存储板
块向云计算、软件及互联网企业扩散，显示市场对AI 的关注点逐步转向企业能
否利用 AI 实现收入增长和利润改善。半导体板块内部明显分化，部分热门存储
股票在财报后大幅下跌， 说明高估值和过度拥挤的交易仍容易受到盈利预期调整
的冲击。 
上周WTI 原油下跌约9%，中东局势继续主导能源市场。周初， 伊朗与阿曼就
霍尔木兹海峡通航安排取得进展，拟议方案包括由伊朗管理进入波斯湾的航道、
阿曼管理驶出航道，并向船舶收取服务费，推动国际油价快速回落。此后，货轮
遇袭、 红海航运风险以及协议细节的不确定性促使油价反弹。 周末伊朗进一步提
出重新开放海峡的前置条件，美伊之间仍存在明显分歧。

经济数据方面， 美国7 月非农就业人数减少2.3 万人， 明显低于市场预期的
增加 8 万人；5 月和 6 月新增就业人数合计下修 10.3 万人。失业率小幅下降至
4.1%，但劳动参与率也进一步下降至61.4%，较年初下降0.7 个百分点，表明失
业率下降部分源于劳动力退出，并不代表就业状况改善。未来若劳动参与率出现
反弹， 可能带动失业率上升。 同时平均时薪环比增速下降到0.1%， 意味着相关性
较高的核心服务通胀或将进一步降温。7 月 ISM 制造业 PMI 升至 55.6，为 2022
年5 月以来最高； 服务业PMI 为54.1， 经济活动和新订单仍保持扩张， 但服务业
就业指数降至 47.4，价格指数升至 70.3。整体来看，美国整体需求和生产活动
仍具韧性， 但企业招聘明显放缓。疲软的非农数据显著降低了市场对美联储9 月
加息的预期，美债收益率出现回落。
